Then `PlayerCombat.Engage` makes sure both stand on opposite sides of one encounter, power is drawn and the shot is booked immediately through `Gunnery.Shoot` on `Universe.Rng` (like `OreChunks.TryFire`). Reload timers live in the participant (`Timers`), run down in the tick (`CombatSystem.Fire` with fire off for the player — the `PlayerTactic` never fires on its own, holds the chosen target and keeps the player's power mode) and are saved. `State(...)` gives the bay state: NoTarget (none/out of range) → NoAmmo → Reloading → NoPower → OutsideArc → Ready (`BayState.NoPower`/`NoAmmo` are new). `WeaponBays.For(u, ship)`: the mining bays as before (the mining laser only with `Mining` capability or without combat weapons) plus one bay `w:<slot>` per combat weapon.

**Player as a combat party (`PlayerCombat`).** `Engage`: join the player's running encounter (target joins the other side), join the target's running encounter (e.g. helping against a raider), or open a new encounter of the new cause `EncounterCause.Assault` (player side 0, target side 1 with tactic by role: police/raider/trader). Shooting a ship that does not belong to an outlaw reports the crime **assault** at the sector owner (`Wanted.Report`, heat 3 = level 3 at once); a police patrol of the sector joins the victim with the raid response chance (one draw). `Finish` for Assault: bounty (`Wanted.BountyOn`) for every destroyed outlaw ship to the player side, every other kill another assault report; no `RaidResolved`. `TraderTactic` also surrenders to the player (`raided` = an enemy with raider **or** player tactic); `Surrender` with the player as taker publishes `CargoDropped` per good (the view turns it into ore-chunk-like pickups for the tractor beam; not persisted — wrecks come with Phase 26). `CombatParticipant.Owner` (saved) keeps the owner for bounty/wanted after destruction. `NextEnemy` (enemies of the own fight by distance, then raiders in the sector, cyclic), `PositionOf` (real combat position or interpolated trip position), `IsHostile`, `Withdraw` (used by `OfflineCatchUp`: an ongoing fight of the player ends before the replay). **Trade refusal:** `TradeActions.Refusal` — the active player ship cannot trade at a station whose owner faction has it wanted at ≥ `TradeRefusalWantedLevel` (3); `Buy/Sell/SellAll/MaxBuyable/Sellable` return nothing, the `trade` offers carry amount 0 with the reason as note, `Execute` refuses with it (jettison stays allowed).

**Raid on the active ship.** The warning stays (`PendingEncounter`, 15 s); during it the raider really flies towards the player in the player sector (`PirateSystem.ApproachPlayer`, up to `RaidApproachDistance` 60). When it is due, `Engage.StartRaid` with the victim tactic `player` (spatial). Escape: docking or jumping (the combat step sees `DockedAt`/another sector/a transit and lets the player leave as `Fled`), or flying out of every enemy's reach (checked for the active player regardless of its move). `RaidResolved.Player` is true for it and carries a player message (destroyed, raider destroyed + bounty, escaped, raiders broke off). The hull floor is gone (`Join` no longer raises player courage, `Shoot` has no floor); the abstract resolver keeps its floors and is used only with `combatRounds` off.

**Loss of the active ship (`Simulation/ShipLoss.cs`, decision 1).** `Engage.Destroy` of the active ship calls `ShipLoss.LoseActive` before the departure: landing station = nearest reachable (fewest jumps, then distance; player profile, empty) **own** station with a hangar, else nearest **neutral** one (`IsNeutral`: owner relation to the player above `HostileRelation`, not wanted ≥ refusal level there), else any with a hangar, else any station. There: an own ship docked there (non-rental first), else a regular rental if the type lists `rental` and `Hangar.RentIssue` is null, else an **emergency rental** (`EmergencyRentalModel`, empty = `RentalModel`; no fee, no deposit, ignores the rental limit; daily rent runs and is settled on return as for any rental). Then `PlayerFleet.Activate`, `Departures.RemoveShip(…, Destroyed, detail)` (cargo gone, money stays) and `PlayerShipLost(lost, station, new ship, how, message)`. Collisions that destroy the hull use the same rule (`Main.Respawn`, detail "collision"). Non-active player fleet ships can now be destroyed too (squadron succession as in Phase 12).

**Docked place after a fight (Phase 24 review).** `Join` remembers `WasDockedAt` (never for the active ship; saved, hashed when set); `Leave` restores exactly that dock and only if the ship is still at its dock point — no new dock (and berth fee) from merely ending near a station. `Leave` no longer resets the active ship's power mode.

**`rearm` service (`Services/RearmService.cs`).** Strict keys `rearm:fill`, `rearm:10`, `rearm:30`; available where the station holds the ammo good and `ammoActive` is on; buys through `Rearm.AtStation` (new optional `maxUnits`) at the market price from the wallet of the ship; failures change nothing and publish nothing; `ServicePerformed` on success. Listed by shipyard, trade station and police station. `Rearm.FromCargo`/`FromCargoIssue` move ammo carried as cargo into the magazine (also in flight; event `AmmoTransferred`).

**Content.** Class `fighter` "Jäger" (S, light kit, hull 70, mass 5, slots reactor/engine/2 bow weapons ±45°/shield/storage/utility) and model `falcon` "Falke" (reactor Mk2, engine S Mk2, laser + massdriver, shield, `power_bank_s`, magazine; no jobs = not bought by the AI). The warden was already buyable for the player; raiders stay restricted. Shipyard shelf `power_bank_s` [1, 2]. Start package `fighter` "Jäger" (Falke, 400 Cr). Arrays appended at the end so earlier tests that take the first class/model keep working. `SaveGameJson.AfterV17Keys` += `fighter`, `falcon`; the migration base also drops late classes, start options of late models, the `rearm` service and the yard's bank shelf (`AfterV17Stocks`).

**View (Godot).** `Game/CombatController` (no rules, only target/auto-fire/warning state): ships are tappable (`SectorView.ShipMarkers`), button "Nächster Gegner" (160×56), target card line with shield/hull (red name when hostile), warning "BESCHUSS! ◀ von links …" for 3 s after a shot on the player (direction relative to the nose), hit flash. Weapon bays: up to six buttons (60 px high, four in the right column, more to the left), "Alle Feuer" also fires ready combat bays, toggle "Dauerfeuer" fires every ready combat bay each frame. Target lock (camera) prefers the selected ship = combat camera. `CargoDropped` becomes pickups in the player's chunk simulation. `PlayerShipLost`: explosion, toast, `EnterActiveShip` at the landing station (dock menu). Hull regeneration in flight pauses while engaged. Dock area "Reparatur" gets a "Munition" section from `rearm` offers (`DockAreas`: area available with `rearm` alone); asset ship detail: "Ins Magazin" for ammo in the hold. Selftest stages 54–62 (falcon in Alpha, raider, "Nächster Gegner", target card, bay shots booked on `Universe.Rng` with bank drain, ammo use and reload, auto-fire until the raider dies → bounty without crime, assault on a trader → wanted level 3 and trade refusal, loss of the falcon in a raid → landing in an own/rental ship at a station with the dock menu, `rearm` through the screen incl. a refused key). `--ui-shots … --ui-shots-phase25` adds 63–65 (combat HUD with target and bays, fight with hit warning, repair area with ammo).

**Persistence.** Additive (save stays v17): `EncounterSave.Spatial`, `CombatParticipantSave.Pos` (x, y, dir x, dir y; only spatial), `WasDockedAt`, `Owner`; new rules in the saved `EconomyRules`. `UniverseHash` mixes positions only for spatial encounters, `Owner` only for spatial/assault encounters and `WasDockedAt` only when set — pure NPC line fights hash exactly as in Phase 24.

**Tests.** New `tests/SpaceSim.Tests/Phase25Tests.cs` (22 test methods, 24 cases with the autopilot theory; plus 2 for the additional step below): player shot = same rule with exactly two draws and deterministic, reload/arc/range, no power/no ammo without any state change, overcharge with a bank, bays per weapon, autopilot harness (approach band from three starts with speed and turn limits, flee, hold, determinism), spatial positions written to the ship, hashed and a mid-fight save → load → 300 ticks bit-identical, line ↔ space transition keeping distances, raid on the player as a real encounter (raider approaches during the warning, docking escapes), no hull floor → loss lands at a station with money kept and cargo gone, landing rule (wanted/neutral/own station/never stuck), own ship at the landing station, regular rental vs emergency rental (also at a station without rental), fleet ship destroyed with squadron succession, assault = crime + trade refusal + trader surrender as `CargoDropped`, raider kill = bounty without crime, `rearm` strict keys and bookings + ammo from cargo, yard sells falcon and warden but not the corsair + start package, docked place only restored when it was docked before, offline catch-up ends the player's fight, and **the NPC world without spatial combat and without the bank shelf reproduces the Phase 24 hash 75532A3F54AD3D9A** (default seed, 20000 ticks). Adjusted on purpose: service registry/type service lists (`rearm`), class/model counts 14/25, start packages (+`fighter`), `EconomyFixTests` (every module now has a shelf consumer: the "module without consumer" assertion is gone; the production-pause test saturates the yard's bank shelves), `Phase24Tests.Andockplatz…` runs outside the player sector (spatially the patrol leaves the dock point), `SecurityTests.Ueberfall_AufSpieler…` checks the old floor only with `combatRounds` off, `StationReviewTests.Langlauf_Umwidmung…` (after the review: spatial combat on again, accepts a retirement without conversion). No test removed. 668 tests green (with the additional step), selftest OK.

**Balance (fresh Release build; before = Phase 24 tree from `git archive HEAD`, same runs).** All runs "Preise im Zielbereich", exit 0. Default 20000 (run twice, identical hash 61C8EA8E056C5CB6): trade/mine 26.1/22.2 → 25.9/22.0, loss rate 0.44 → 0.55 %, victims destroyed 0 → 1, raids 55 → 67, raiders 2+2 → 3+2 (treasuries 5.4k/5.4k → 8.5k/9.7k), HINWEIS 0 → 0, 0.31 → 0.33–0.34 ms/tick. 60000: 26.2/21.7 → 26.2/22.0, 0.39 → 0.54 %, victims 1 → 2, raids per 20000 ticks 55/43/46 → 67/44/48, 2+2 raiders both, HINWEIS 0 → 0, 0.211 → 0.214 ms/tick. Seeds 1–4 at 20000: trade 26.0/26.4/26.0/26.3 → 26.5/26.0/26.2/26.5, mine 22.2/22.0/22.5/22.4 → 22.4/22.0/22.3/22.4, loss 0.45/0.89/0.79/0.48 → 0.61/0.57/0.61/0.70 %, raids 69/71/68/57 → 58/61/71/71, victims 0/0/0/0 → 0/0/3/1, HINWEIS 3/1/1/1 → 1/1/0/0, ms/tick +2–5 %. 300000 (`--every 50000`): 26.0/22.1 → 26.2/22.2, 0.48 → 0.53 %, victims 7 → 5, raids 714 → 751 (44–67 per 20000; before 29–57 with one group briefly at 0 raiders), two raiders per group almost throughout (one at the very end), HINWEIS 0 → 1 (a wafer fab with empty cash 35 % of the time), 0.166 → 0.167 ms/tick. The NPC change comes only from spatial fights in the player sector (Alpha in the headless run) and the bank shelf (see the hash test).

**Known weaknesses.** The headless player sector (Alpha) makes NPC fights there spatial, which shifts outcomes slightly (spatial raiders fire only inside the bow arc, flee straight) — the loss rate rose ≈ 0.1 pp. The combat autopilot ignores obstacles and gates (no NPC escape by jump or by reaching a station). Dropped cargo from a surrendering victim is a view-only pickup (lost on save/load). The player cannot surrender to raiders (only fight or escape). Police join an assault only at its start (one response draw); a wanted player is still handled by the Phase 11 interception, not by police attacking him. NPC fleets of the player in the player sector are steered by the autopilot like NPCs. Bay names of long modules overflow the 176-px button. Overcharge exists only for ships with a bank (fighter); police/raiders have no storage slot.

**Additional step of Phase 25: every trader is armed (user request, measured separately after the Phase 25 acceptance).** Every model with the job `trade` carries a `weapon_laser_s` (energy, no ammunition logistics): `unit_trader_100/60` and `behemoth` through a new turret weapon slot appended at the end of their classes (`unit_trader`, `heavy_freighter`), `mule`, `hauler`, `tinker` in the existing weapon slot of the light freighter (turret). Power balance holds (unit trader 6/10, Mule 7/10, Tinker 8/10, Behemoth 7/10; peak 1 within the surplus). To keep cargo **and** speed unchanged the classes became 2 lighter (the laser's mass): `unit_trader` 10 → 8, `light_freighter` 16 → 14, `heavy_freighter` 30 → 28 — unit traders still fly at exactly 8. Founder traders get the laser from their model; AI new builds (model choice, clone and the kit-only fallback, which always copies the model's fit) are armed by construction. Mining ships are not armed; the player skiffs (Nomad, Pip) were left unchanged (their class has no weapon slot; adding one would change the skiff fits — kept small). The separate variants `mule_armed`/`hauler_armed` were **removed** (identical or redundant now); the knob stays as „heavily armed“: new rule `ArmedTraderBaseline` (default world 6 ≈ one laser, code default 0 = Phase 24 meaning) — `FleetSystem.ArmamentBonus` only values armament above that baseline, which no default model exceeds. Migration base: `AfterV17Fits` restores the v17 fits of the six trade models, `AfterV17WeaponSlot` removes the appended slot, `AfterV17ClassMass` restores the masses. Tests: `Phase25Tests.JederHaendler_…` (every trade model has weapon power > 0, `Combat` capability, valid power balance, cargo unchanged, no armed variants, miners unarmed; founder traders and every AI trader and trade new build after 20000 ticks armed); the Phase 24 hash test also reverts this step (classes, fits, the two variants, baseline 0) and still reproduces 75532A3F54AD3D9A. Adjusted on purpose: capability expectations of traders (`Cargo | Combat`: `CapabilityTests` ×2, `ShipFitTests`, `SlotModelTests` ×2 incl. fit length/power/peak messages), model count 23, Phase 24 tests (`mule` instead of `mule_armed`, the unarmed-surrender test removes the laser, the ammo test fits the massdriver into a hauler, the armament-bonus test uses baseline 0), `MultiroleTests.Modellwahl_Schuerfluecke…` (the mandatory laser makes the Tinker dearer, so by default the specialist now wins the mining gap; the flexibility rule is checked on a Tinker without the laser), `MiningTests.AsLegacyJson` (a synthetic v2 save no longer carries today's slot content and fits). Balance (before = Phase 25 without this step): all runs "Preise im Zielbereich"; default 20000 (twice, hash C750CF318B5E2318): trade/mine 25.9/22.0 → 26.3/22.5, loss 0.55 → 0.58 %, raids 67 → 65, 2+2 raiders (8.5k/9.7k → 9.2k/8.5k), trader bankruptcies 3 → 1, HINWEIS 0 → 1, armed AI traders 24 of 24; 60000: 26.2/22.0 → 26.3/22.5, 0.54 → 0.44 %, raids per 20000 ticks 67/44/48 → 65/53/37, HINWEIS 0 → 0; seeds 1–4 at 20000: loss 0.61/0.57/0.61/0.70 → 0.59/0.98/0.69/0.86 %, raids 58/61/71/71 → 66/75/71/75, HINWEIS 1/1/0/0 → 0/0/1/1 (seed 2 at 60000: 0.83 %); 300000: 26.2/22.2 → 26.4/21.9, 0.53 → 0.43 %, raids 751 → 737, raiders destroyed 23 → 11, trader bankrupt/unprofitable 6/5 → 1/0, HINWEIS 1 → 0, 0.167 → 0.158 ms/tick. Mk1 reactor/engine/laser 0.8–0.94×, steel 1.05–1.24×, ammo 0.88–0.94× — nothing drained. Weakness: raiders now mostly hit unarmed miners (aversion to armed victims), so the loss rate spreads more between seeds (0.43–0.98 %; a test with `raiderArmedAversion` 0.3 did not narrow it and was reverted); multirole Tinkers are bought less often (0–2 per run).

**Correction after review (Phase 25).**
1. *Wanted in unowned sectors:* an assault was reported only to the sector owner, so Nebelmark/Abgrund were crime-free. `Wanted.AssaultAuthorities(u, sector, victimOwner)` = victim's faction ∪ sector owner; if both are empty (free victim in an unowned sector) the owners of the adjacent sectors (breadth-first over gates until a ring has an owner); `Wanted.ReportAssault` reports at all of them (attack start and every non-outlaw kill).
2. *Lost rentals:* the lost rental still counted against `RentalMaxPerPlayer`, so a player with money got an emergency ship. `Hangar.RentIssue/Rent` take an `ignore` ship; `Hangar.SettleLostRental` (called by `ShipLoss.LoseActive`) forfeits the whole deposit to the rental station and charges the rent beyond it from the wallet, even below zero (event `RentalLost`, shown in the loss message). Emergency rentals (`Hangar.IsEmergencyRental` = no deposit) pay their full rent on return or loss, if necessary as debt (negative wallet) — no free cycle.
3. *Station protection:* attacking a ship inside `StationSafeRadius` of a station makes that station's faction police in the sector join for sure (no random draw) and reports the heavier crime `assault_station` (heat 4.5, fine 1500); no hard ban.
4. *Landing:* `ShipLoss.Landing` returns `LandingChoice(Station, Reachable, Neutral, Own)`; reachable stations (route with the person's profile, closed gates excluded) always win, a station behind closed gates is only the last resort and the message says so; when the person is wanted at the landing station's faction, the message names the station with a court (`ShipLoss.FineHint`).
5. *Combat participants inside rocks* (4.7 % of the reviewer's samples): `CombatSpace.Clear` pushes a position out of asteroids and station bodies (same circle rule as `Collision.Resolve`, ship radius + 1, deterministic, no random draw) on the line → space transition and when a spatial NPC participant leaves a fight.
6. *Loss rate:* cause — with every trader armed, raiders almost only attack unarmed miners, nearly always win (0–2 raiders lost per 20000 ticks) and run their treasury at the cap, so raids kept coming at full rate. Lever: default world `raidRatePerTick` 0.008 → 0.0065 (miners stay unarmed). Seeds 1–8 at 20000: 0.59/0.98/0.69/0.86/0.75/0.72/0.52/0.74 % (mean 0.73) → 0.34/0.78/0.47/0.49/0.57/0.37/0.47/0.63 % (mean 0.52), raids 64–80 → 58–71, raiders 2–3/2 per group; default 20000 (hash twice 8861B10EE586BAA4) 0.58 → 0.40 %, 60000 0.44 → 0.39 %, seed 2 at 60000 0.83 → 0.54 %, 300000 0.43 → 0.37 % (raids per 20000 ticks 37–61, two raiders per group almost throughout, treasuries 2–9k), gate 17 closed at 50000 to 200000 0.41 → 0.52 % (exit 2 only for the split, trader bankruptcies 12 → 3). All runs "Preise im Zielbereich", capacity trade 26.0–26.5 / mine 20.5–22.5, HINWEIS 0–2 per run (known early ore-starved copper smelter/steelworks), 0.155–0.33 ms/tick.
7. `StationReviewTests.Langlauf_Umwidmung…` runs with spatial combat again; on that path the titanium works is retired without conversion and the lifecycle builds the water plant on the freed slot — the test accepts `ForType` "" or `water_plant`, all other assertions unchanged.
8. Long weapon names are shortened to their last word in the bay ("Geschütz"), bays are 56 px high with a 60 px step so "Alle Feuer" ends 16 px above the slider; the mid-fight roundtrip test also moves the player ship every tick.
9. **Collisions destroy the ship for real** (user decision): a hard impact that empties the hull goes through `ShipLoss.LoseActive` (detail "collision") exactly like a combat loss — ship and cargo gone, money stays, landing at a station.
Tests: `Phase25Tests` +6 (authorities in unowned/owned sectors, station protection with exactly the expected draws, lost rental settlement and no false emergency ship, emergency rent as debt, landing reachable vs last resort and wanted hint, clearing out of rocks); the Phase 24 hash test also restores the code raid rate. 674 tests green, selftest OK.

## Phase 26 (English summary): damage and salvage — module damage, fire, module repair, no free repair, paid NPC repair, wrecks and cargo containers, daily rental rent

Fourth stage of the combat plan (`docs/KAMPF.md` 3.3, 4.4, 4.5, decision 4). Master switch `EconomyRules.DamageActive` (default true; false = Phase 25 behaviour: no draws for module damage, no fire, no wrecks, parked NPC ships regenerate their hull for free).

**Module damage (`Combat/ModuleDamage.cs`).** Called from the shared shot rule `Gunnery.Shoot` (NPC fire and player shots alike) after every hull hit. Only while the target is below `ModuleDamageHullShare` (0.5) of its max hull (hull > 0, ship with a model) a hull hit draws **exactly three** numbers (`DrawsPerHullHit`: module hit yes/no with `ModuleHitChance` 0.35, which fitted slot, ignition), independent of the outcome; every other hit draws none. A module hit lowers the slot's condition by `ModuleDamageBase` (0.12) + hull damage ÷ max hull × `ModuleDamageScale` (2) through `ModuleDamage.Damage` (event `ModuleDamaged`, `Failed` when it crosses `ModuleFailCondition`). Phase 23's failure rule applies (reactor/engine at half output, never off), with one deliberate change: a **failed cargo module no longer holds cargo** — `ModuleDamage.SetCondition` recomputes stats, sets `CargoCapacity = Stats.Cargo` and `Spill` drops whatever no longer fits (form rule as `FreeVolumeForForm`, highest good id first) as a saved cargo container at the ship's position — never an invalid hold.

**Fire (`Combat/FireSystem.cs`, decision of the user: fire with an extinguishing chance, no crew, no button).** An undamaged-not-failed module that is hit can ignite with `IgniteChance` = `FireChance` (0.08) × (1 + (1 − condition) × `FireConditionFactor` 2) × `FireFlammableFactor` (2) for reactors, engines and magazines. `ShipSystemsState.Burning` (sorted slot list, saved in `ShipSystemsSave.Burning`, hashed only when present). `FireSystem` (Combat module, after `ShipSystemsSystem`) iterates only the recovery index (`ShipSystems.Recovers` now includes burning ships; `ShipSystemsSystem` keeps them in the index), so undamaged ships cost nothing. A docked ship extinguishes at once ("docked"); every fire round (`FireRoundTicks` 4 = 1 s) draws exactly one number per burning module: below `ExtinguishChance` = `FireExtinguishChance` (0.12) + `ShipStats.Extinguish` it is out ("extinguished"), otherwise the module loses `FireConditionLoss` (0.04) — a failed module ends the fire ("burnt_out") — and the hull `FireHullDamageShare` (0.5 %) of max hull (a fire alone never destroys a ship: hull stays ≥ 1). It burns on after the combat ends. Repair ends a fire ("repaired"). Events `FireStarted`/`FireEnded`.

**Content.** Modules `wet_suppressor_s` "Löschanlage" (utility S, stat `extinguish` 0.35; steel/electronics/hull plates 1 each) and `wreck_grapple_s` "Bergungsgreifer" (tool S, grants `Salvage`; steel 2, electronics 1, hull plates 1) — keys sort after the weapon modules so all module good ids stay; made by the systems works through the factory mechanism, yard shelves [1,2]. Headquarters, sector HQs and police stations list `repair` and hold stock-only shelves hull plates [8,8] / electronics [4,4] — with the plating works and yards every map part of the default world that has an economy has a repair station (also Aurelia behind gate 17; Abgrund has only the hideout). `SaveGameJson.AfterV17Keys` += both modules, `AfterV17Stocks` += the six shelves, `AfterV17RepairTypes` removes the new `repair` entries from the migration base.

**Repair (`Services/RepairService.cs`).** Offers `repair:full`/`repair:half` (hull, as before), `repair:module:<slot>` per damaged module and `repair:all` (hull and all modules). Module material per missing condition point and slot size step (S 1 … XL 4): `ModuleRepairPlates` (1) hull plates + `ModuleRepairElectronics` (0.5) electronics from the station stock, price = material × market price × `LaborFactor` 1.5 (as before), partial when stock or money is short; strict keys (`repair:module:` + plain digits only); a repaired module stops burning. The shared rule `RepairService.Perform(u, station, ship, hullShare, slots, maxCost)` books material, money (station ← `WalletOf(ship)`), condition and publishes `ShipRepaired`; the service adds `ServicePerformed`. **No free repair any more:** the view no longer restores the hull on docking or regenerates it in flight (only with `DamageActive` off); docking puts out fires. The outfitter refuses to remove or swap a damaged module (`OutfitterService.DamagedIssue`: "erst reparieren") — removed modules are condition-less goods, so removal would erase the damage for free.

**NPC repair (`Simulation/NpcRepair.cs`, new `RepairModule` after Jobs, `NpcRepairSystem` interval 10).** The free parked regeneration of `SecuritySystem` runs only with `DamageActive` off. Traders and miners (own wallet) repair through `RepairService.Perform` at stations with `repair`: on every arrival (`TraderSystem`/`MinerSystem.Arrive` → `NpcRepair.OnArrival`) and while parked there; budget `NpcRepairBudgetShare` of their credits, light damage only from money above `NpcRepairReserve` (first pass 0.3/20000 — NPCs repaired only serious damage; since the correction after review 0.1/1000 with the cheaper repair price `RepairPlatesPerHull` 0.02, see below). Seriously damaged traders/miners (hull < `NpcRepairSeekHull` 0.35, a failed module or a fire; ≥ 50 Cr, empty hold) fly to the nearest reachable station that can repair now before planning their next round (`NpcRepair.Seek`, called by `TraderSystem`/`MinerSystem` right before `PickOrder` and by `NpcRepairSystem`; `GoToOrder`, handled on arrival like a fuel stop) — without the hook in the planners miners kept mining at < 35 % hull (11.7 % of miner samples serious, the Gamma colony short of luxury goods 36 % of the time instead of 21 %). A ship with a failed module or a fire (`NpcRepair.Crippled`) repairs its modules first and may spend its whole wallet on them (`RepairService.Perform(…, modulesFirst)`). Police and pirates repair at their base from the owner's treasury (`PirateSystem.ServiceAtBase` → `NpcRepair.AtBase`: hull at `BaseRepairPerHull`, modules at `BaseModuleRepairCost` (25) per point and size step, fires out; `ShipRepaired` with `AtBase`); police also return home with a failed module or a fire (`NpcRepair.Crippled`). Faction construction ships are repaired by their faction from the treasury when not working (`TreasuryUpkeep` "builder"). Player fleet ships under command: the emergency level now also triggers on "Modulschaden" (failed module or fire) and `ReturnExecutor.Service` repairs `repair:all`. Repair costs are business results for the Phase 22 profitability measurement (nothing neutralised).

**Wrecks and containers (`Model/Wreck.cs`, `Simulation/Wrecks.cs`, `WreckSystem` in the Combat module, interval 50).** `Universe.Wrecks` (list in creation order, `NextWreckId`; saved additively as `SaveGame.Wrecks`/`NextWreckId`, hashed only when a wreck ever existed). Every ship destroyed through `Departures.RemoveShip` (reason a) leaves a wreck at its (combat) position: `WreckCargoShare` (0.5) of its cargo without a draw and, per fitted module, exactly two draws (survives with `WreckModuleChance` 0.4, rest condition = condition at destruction × U[`WreckConditionMin` 0.3, 1]); nothing to take = no wreck. Surrendered cargo taken by the player and cargo spilled from a failed cargo module become **saved cargo containers** (`WreckKind.Container`, replacing Phase 25's view-only pickups; `CargoDropped` is still published). Wrecks expire after `WreckTicks` (12000), containers after `ContainerTicks` (4800) (`WreckExpired`); empty ones vanish. Salvage rules: cargo can be pulled by any ship within `SalvageRange` (30) (`Wrecks.TakeCargo`, form rule, the player through the tractor beam); modules only with `Capability.Salvage` (`Wrecks.TakeModule`) and only from a rest condition ≥ `SalvageMinCondition` (0.5) — **decision:** a salvaged module lands in the hold as an ordinary module good (fungible, sells and fits like new); the threshold is the value discount (worse modules are scrap and stay in the wreck). NPC path: a surviving raider takes the cargo of its destroyed victim's wreck (`Wrecks.LootByRaider`, fence like loot, capped by `PirateTreasuryCap`; rule `RaidersLootWrecks`), otherwise wrecks just expire. Events `WreckCreated`, `WreckSalvaged`, `WreckExpired`.

**Daily rental rent (review of Phase 25).** `HangarSystem.ChargeRent` charges `RentalPerDay` for every started rental day not yet billed (`Ship.RentPaidDays`, saved additively and hashed when set) from the owner's wallet to the rental station — below zero if necessary (event `RentCharged` with the debt part); return and loss settle only the open days (`Hangar.OpenRent`). Debts are visible: the flight HUD shows "Schulden: n Cr" in red when the wallet is negative, "Meine Fraktion" likewise (`Hangar.Debt`).

**Robust clearing (review of Phase 25).** `CombatSpace.Clear` resolves up to 16 passes, then searches a free spot on rings around the position (16 fixed directions, steps of 4, no random draw) and bounds positions to the sector's combat area `CombatArenaRadius` (400); `CombatSpace.Free` checks the result.

**UI.** Flight HUD: red fire warning under the wanted line (`DamageInfo.FireWarning`: modules, extinguishing chance per round, "Andocken löscht"), debts in the credits line. Dock area "Reparatur": damage list (`DamageInfo.For`: hull, modules with condition, failed, fire) above the repair rows; module rows in condition percent, "Alles reparieren" with the total; rows are two-line (offer, then amount/price) and the area scrolls. Outfitter: slot buttons show condition/failed/BRENNT and a damage summary ("erst reparieren"). Asset ship detail: damage list (up to three module rows) above the cargo. `SectorView.SyncWrecks`: wrecks as dark debris, containers as orange boxes with labels (not obstacles); command-centre sector view: grey crosses/orange squares. Tapping a wreck/container selects it (`Game/SalvageController`, toast with `DamageInfo.WreckLine`); the tractor beam pulls cargo from the selected/nearest wreck in range (6 units/s); new bay "Bergen" (`WeaponBays.Salvager`, only with `Salvage`) salvages the best module per tap. Bay names: compound weapon words are shortened to their first part ("Laserkanone" → "Laser", `Main.ShortBayName`). Selftest stages 63–66 (fire in flight and HUD warning, no free repair on docking and docking extinguishes, damage list and `repair:all` through the screen incl. a refused key, ship with grapple: wreck drawn, module salvaged, tractor empties the wreck, HUD debts). `--ui-shots … --ui-shots-phase26` adds 66 (repair area with module rows), 67 (ship detail with damage list), 68 (flight with wreck, container and fire warning).

**Balance tool.** Lines "Schaden" (module damage, failures, fires started and how they ended), "Reparaturen" (service repairs NPC/player, cost, hull plates and electronics, base repairs from treasuries, modules repaired, missing hull), "Händlerpleiten und Reparatur" (bankrupt traders that had paid for repairs), "Wracks" (wrecks/containers created, cargo and modules, salvaged by raiders/player, expired), "Reparaturmaterial" (hull plates, electronics, the two new modules and the laser cannon, dense sample).

**Persistence.** Additive (save stays v17): `ShipSystemsSave.Burning`, `ShipSave.RentPaidDays`, `SaveGame.Wrecks`/`NextWreckId` (`WreckSave`, `WreckModuleSave`); new rules in the saved `EconomyRules`.

**Tests.** New `tests/SpaceSim.Tests/Phase26Tests.cs` (17): module damage only below half hull with exactly three draws (none above the threshold or with the switch off), failure (reactor half, weapon off), damage through hits; a failed cargo module spills into a saved container and leaves a valid hold; fire (ignition chance by condition and flammability, progress without combat, one draw per round, extinguishing chance with the suppressor, chance 1 puts it out, docking extinguishes); repair service for modules (strict keys without state change or event, material and price, repair ends a fire, outfitter refuses damaged modules); no free regeneration of parked NPCs, paid repair at a station with bookings, budget share, no light repair for poor ships, switch off = old regeneration; NPC repair path (seriously damaged trader flies to the nearest repair station and books the repair; every map part with an economy in the default world has a repair station even with gate 17 or 27 closed); police repair hull and modules at the base from the treasury; wreck content (cargo share, exactly two draws per fitted module, expiry), salvage with and without grapple, tractor cargo only, scrap threshold, `WreckSystem` expiry, no wreck and no draws with the switch off; raiders loot the wreck cargo into the treasury; containers, wrecks and fire survive save → load bit-identical incl. 600 ticks of continuation; surrender to the player becomes a saved container; daily rental rent incl. debts and no double charge on loss; `CombatSpace.Clear` leaves no overlap and stays inside the arena for 40 rocks per sector; damage list and fire warning; 40000-tick runs on the default and classic worlds (loss rate < 1 %, holds never over capacity, fires only on fitted slots, wrecks never stale, repairs happen, police minimum and seats, trade/mine capacity ≥ 80 %, ≤ 10 % of economy ships seriously damaged). The Phase 25 hash test additionally reverts Phase 26 (`Phase26Tests.RevertPhase26`: switch off, new modules/shelves and the government repair service removed) and still reproduces the Phase 24 hash 75532A3F54AD3D9A. Adjusted on purpose: `Phase23Tests` (failed cargo module loses its capacity), `SlotModelTests` (26 modules), `StationServiceConfigTests` (HQ/sector HQ/police list `repair`), `ActorTests.Vermoegensabgabe…` (removes the new repair shelves of official stations in both worlds — they buy from station cash, which without a treasury depends on the upkeep and fed back through trade), `FleetCommandTests.Leihschiff…` (rent is charged daily; the return only refunds the deposit), `Phase20Tests.Panik_AlphaZu…` (`MaxActiveSites` 0 after the cut so the regular lifecycle cannot answer the water scarcity before the panic on the changed random path). No test removed. 691 tests green, selftest OK.

**Balance (fresh Release build; before = Phase 25 tree from `git archive HEAD`, same runs).** All runs "Preise im Zielbereich" (exit 0; the gate run exit 2 only for the map split, as before). Default 20000 (twice, identical hash 2676C583E6FC0BB4): trade/mine 26.4/22.5 → 26.5/22.5, loss rate 0.40 → 0.39 %, raids 61 → 67, victims destroyed 0 → 2, pirates 2/9051 + 2/6360 → 3/9962 + 2/5098 (raiders/treasury), trader bankruptcies 1 → 0, HINWEIS 1 → 1, 0.32 → 0.35 ms/tick; 190 module damages (5 failures), 15 fires (8 extinguished, 5 burnt out, 2 docked), 31 NPC service repairs for 22 324 Cr (126 hull plates, 6.8 electronics) + 31 base repairs (2 623 Cr), 2 wrecks + 1 container. 60000: 26.4/22.5 → 26.2/22.5, 0.39 → 0.37 %, bankruptcies 2 → 2, HINWEIS 0 → 0, 0.204 → 0.217 ms/tick. Seeds 1–8 at 20000: loss 0.34/0.78/0.47/0.49/0.57/0.37/0.47/0.63 → 0.46/0.75/0.61/0.51/0.77/0.60/0.36/0.54 % (all < 0.85 %), trade 26.0–26.5 → 26.3–27.0, mine 21.9–22.5 → 22.3–22.5, bankruptcies 0/0/0/0/0/2/0/1 → 0/0/0/0/2/2/2/0, HINWEIS 1/2/0/0/1/1/0/0 → 1/2/1/3/1/0/0/2. Seed 2 at 60000: 0.54 → 0.70 %, bankruptcies 1 → 1. 300000 (`--every 50000`): 26.4/22.5 → 26.3/22.5, 0.37 → 0.41 %, raids 658 → 713, victims 2 → 9, two raiders per group throughout, treasuries at the end 5.3k/8.4k → 9.3k/9.9k, **trader bankruptcies 2 → 14 (+3 unprofitable)**, HINWEIS 0 → 0, 0.156 → 0.169 ms/tick; 1 885 module damages (59 failures), 189 fires (132 extinguished, 45 burnt out, 7 docked, 2 repaired), 315 NPC service repairs for 173 129 Cr (1 109 hull plates, 62 electronics), 447 base repairs (52 600 Cr), 19 wrecks + 6 containers (78 units looted by raiders, 21 expired; 47 modules in wrecks, none salvaged — NPCs do not salvage modules). Gate 17 closed at 50000 to 200000: 0.52 → 0.41 %, bankruptcies 3 → 6, Klingen treasury 9.9k → 0.1k at the end (two raiders). Prices (dense tail): hull plates 1.05–1.06 → 1.04–1.09, electronics 0.93–0.97 → 0.93–1.02, Mk1 reactor/engine/laser 0.77–0.95 → 0.77–0.96 (no module at the lower cap, no new HINWEIS kind), new modules 0.90–0.93, laser cannon 0.80–0.89. Additional 300000-tick runs (before → after): seed 1 bankruptcies 0 → 2, seed 2 1 → 3.

**Known weaknesses.** Trader bankruptcies rise in long runs (default seed 300000: 2 → 14; seeds 1/2: 0/1 → 2/3; 20000/60000 runs unchanged within noise). Measured: with repairs from a 1500-Cr cushion they were 12 per 150000 ticks (repairs ≈ 0.75 Cr/tick from the ship accounts) — now NPCs repair only serious damage, but most bankrupt traders still carry hull damage (11 of 14), none a failed module; the free regeneration hid the cost of combat damage, which now reaches the thin-margin spiral (Phase 22/24) through capital and earlier surrender. NPCs never salvage modules (wrecks expire); the player is the only module salvager, so the module inflow from wrecks is zero for the economy. Salvaged modules count as new goods (scrap threshold only). A fire alone never destroys a ship. Ships with hull damage above the serious threshold fly on unrepaired (by design), so the NPC fleet carries ≈ 1 000–1 900 missing hull points. The pirate treasury of one group can still sink to ≈ 0 in single long runs (gate run). The damage list in the asset detail shows at most three modules.

**Correction after review (Phase 26).**
1. *Trader bankruptcies (cause measured by the reviewer: the repair price — ≈ 8 Cr per hull point, a full trader hull ≈ 885 Cr against ≈ 4000 Cr capital; module damage, repair trips, speed/cargo and combat strength were not the cause).* `RepairPlatesPerHull` 0.05 → **0.02** for player and NPC alike (one price rule; ≈ 3 Cr per hull point at base prices), `NpcRepairBudgetShare` 0.3 → 0.1, `NpcRepairReserve` 20000 → 1000 (with the cheaper repair NPCs also fix medium damage), `NpcRepairSeekHull` stays 0.35 (0.5/0.6 gave more repair trips but not fewer bankruptcies). Calibrated with a probe over 300000 ticks (default/seed 3/seed 4; variants: seek 0.6, reserve 2500 + seek 0.5): first-pass rules → these values bankruptcies 14/8/4 → 2/0/2 (before the later correction steps; the final balance runs below are the reference). `NpcRepair.Seek` was not dead code but almost never triggered (traders rarely fall below 35 %); it now publishes `RepairTripStarted` and the balance tool counts the trips (≈ 20–75 per 20000 ticks).
2. *Soft lock.* New offer `repair:emergency` "Notreparatur (auf Schuld, nur Funktion)" at every station with `repair` whenever the ship is not functional (`RepairService.NeedsEmergency`: failed module, fire or hull below `EmergencyRepairHullShare` 0.25): failed modules up to `ModuleFailCondition` + `EmergencyRepairMargin` (0.05), fires out, hull up to 25 %; price as the normal repair (market price, else base price) × labour; material from the stock as far as available, without stock no material (money still to the station); the wallet may go negative (debts are shown). `repair:all` repairs failed/burning modules first, then the hull, when money is short. Test: a prospector with hull 1, failed laser and cargo module and 0 Cr is functional again and earns money by selling ore.
3. Upgrades of a damaged or burning module are refused ("Modul beschädigt – erst reparieren", `ShipUpgrade.DamagedIssue`; `Attempt` books nothing).
4. `Hangar.SellPrice` × `Hangar.DamageFactor` (hull share × mean condition of the fitted modules).
5. `OfflineCatchUp` extinguishes fires on the active ship before the replay (offline no reaction is possible).
6. `Phase20Tests.Panik_AlphaZu…` is path independent again (a finished water plant outside Alpha — built by the panic or the lifecycle —, no open panic, farms running; if the panic was raised it answered with a water plant); the panic decision itself is checked deterministically in the new `Panik_Entscheidung_WasserwerkFehltImRestteil_Deterministisch` (`Missing`, `PlanFor`, `StartBuild`). The `MaxActiveSites` crutch is gone.
7. Salvaged modules: unchanged (condition is not carried; the scrap threshold is the value discount). Checked that destroying an own ship and salvaging never pays: even with every module surviving at full condition the salvage value stays below the ship's material value (kit and labour are lost) — test `Bergung_KeinLohnenderKreislauf…`.
8. *Faction reserve at start.* Measured: every faction is below its reserve in the first 1000–2200 ticks on all seeds 1–8 (the treasuries start empty and fill from taxes). First tried: start treasuries = reserve (Union 42000, guild 31000, Montanbund 24000) — removed again, because the extra early money shifted the long runs measurably (trader bankruptcies at 300000 ticks default/seed 3: 22/23 with vs 7/10 without). Instead `FactionReserveBuildTicks` (6000): within that time after a faction's creation "below reserve" is no violation (`FactionViability.ReserveBuilding`; the spending rules still keep the reserve); the faction line reads "handlungsfähig – Rücklage im Aufbau (x von y Cr)", later "handlungsfähig – Rücklage unterschritten (…)" (still a violation in the balance measure, which also counts the build-up samples separately).

**Device test (Samsung A15, 2340 × 1080; first test on a real phone).**
- *Throttle/autopilot:* `Throttle.Snap` (dead zone: < 5 % → 0, > 95 % → 1, label "MAX"; `KitSlider.Snap`); switching the autopilot or auto-mining on sets the slider to 0 (a 1–3 % rest used to cancel the autopilot silently), a manual cancel shows "Autopilot aus (manuelle Steuerung)". `KitButton` now draws an active toggle (`On`) as active — before only `State` was drawn, so Autopilot, Auto-Mining, Dauerfeuer and the power-mode buttons never looked active.
- *Auto-mining hang (Pip with separate holds, solid hold full):* the autopilot counted a chunk that no longer fits as "chunks near" and waited forever; now only loadable chunks count (`MiningAutopilot`), it flies to sell; `OreChunks.HoldFull` gives the reason ("Laderaum für Eisenerz voll (fest) – verkaufen oder abwerfen"). Kinematic test `Pip_FesterRaumVoll_BrockenDaneben_FliegtVerkaufenStattZuWarten`.
- *Time-lapse and danger:* raid warning, shots at the player, a fire and the loss of the ship switch the time-lapse to ×1 (`Main.Danger`); important messages (raid warning/outcome, fire, ship loss, time-lapse off) use the new `Hud.Alert` (red panel, large, ≥ 6 s or until tapped); toasts are narrower and wrap (no longer cover the target card).
- *Power modes:* visible active state, a short message with the effect (`ShipSystems.ModeEffect`) and the mode in the power line.
- *Tapping ships:* picking in screen space with ≥ 56 device pixels (`CombatController.PickScreen`), nearest ship wins; every foreign ship has a glowing ring under its marker; "Nächster Gegner" is visible whenever an enemy is in the sector (`FlightHudData.EnemyNear`).
- *Text overlap (systematic):* `UiKit.At` clips every fixed-width label with an ellipsis; `KitButton.Fit` shortens button text/sub/detail to the button (detail line moved inside the button — it was drawn outside on the right); `KitWindow` and dock areas have inner padding; new `Ui/Kit/LabelLayout` places map labels collision-free (sector map, star map), universe nodes fit name and sub-line to their spacing; sector map scale ignores the player (shown at the rim with "(außerhalb)" when far away), two-line legend; star map opaque with opaque nodes; station info service chips wrap and name the service ("Reparatur", "Tanken"), not the dock area; the HUD hit warning moved below the toast area (a toast covered it); assets row columns fit, the active ship reads "im Flug"/"angedockt", "AKTIV" right-aligned inside; "Meine Fraktion" ability rows fit the card; target card lines end before the radar; repair area rows two-line and scrollable (Phase 26). Outdated texts: dock menu footer ("Hülle repariert beim Andocken" → hull and repair hint), repair placeholder text, laser bay "FEUER · nächster Fels" without a chosen target.
- *Wide displays:* `window/stretch/aspect="expand"`; the flight HUD anchors its elements left/centre/right by their position in the 1280 × 720 layout and respects the device safe area (`DisplayServer.GetDisplaySafeArea`); full-screen screens stay 1280 wide, centred over a dark backdrop. 1280 × 720 is unchanged (selftest/UI shots), a second UI-shot run at 2340 × 1080 was checked.
- *Test scenario:* start package `test` "Testszenario (Test)" (`player.startOptions[].scenario`, applied by `Content/StartScenario`): Tinker with massdriver, ammo, magazine and salvage grapple next to Werft Delta (repair, outfitter, shipyard, rearm), engine damaged and burning, a wreck in salvage range, a raider lurking in sight, 3000 Cr. Selftest stage 67 starts it and checks the time-lapse switch-off with an alert.

**Balance (correction; Phase 25 → first pass → correction).** All open runs "Preise im Zielbereich", gate run exit 2 only for the split. Default 20000 (hash 9D55ECC506F36A98 twice): trade/mine 26.5/22.1, loss 0.40 → 0.39 → 0.30 %, trader bankruptcies 1 → 0 → 1, AI new builds 15 → 16, victims lost 0 → 2, mean hull share of AI traders/miners 0.93, 0.35 ms/tick. 60000: loss 0.39 → 0.37 → 0.31 %, bankruptcies 2 → 2 → 7 (all before tick 60000; the 300000 run adds none), builds 16 → 23. Seeds 1–8 at 20000: loss 0.46/0.42/0.47/0.34/0.55/0.61/0.37/0.60 % (Phase 25 0.34–0.78), bankruptcies together 3 (Phase 25: 3), HINWEIS 0–5. 300000 default: loss 0.37 → 0.41 → 0.25 %, bankruptcies 2 → 14 → 7, builds 18 → 25, victims 2 → 5, mean hull 0.83 (8 % of samples seriously damaged), 513 NPC repairs for 151 k Cr + 322 at bases for 33 k, pirates 2/6.3 k + 1/0, 0.172 ms/tick (Phase 25 0.156). Seed 3 300000: bankruptcies 0 → 10, builds 17 → 34, victims 3 → 7, loss 0.38 → 0.26 %, hull 0.82, pirates end with one raider and an empty treasury each; seed 4 300000: bankruptcies 2 → 0, builds 21 → 15, victims 7 → 6, loss 0.43 → 0.30 %. Gate run 17 closed (200000): loss 0.52 → 0.41 → 0.34 %, bankruptcies 3 → 6 → 8. Hull plates 1.05×, electronics 0.96×, Mk1 modules 0.81–0.85×. Reference on the same code with `damageActive` off (probe, 300000): default/seed 3 bankruptcies 6/1 — the default seed's bankruptcies are mostly path noise, seed 3's are a real but small extra cost of paid repair (bankrupt traders spent ≈ 330 Cr each on repairs over their life; most had full hull when they went broke — the thin-load spiral of Phases 20/22).

**Tests (correction).** `Phase26Tests` +9 (reserve build-up, emergency repair soft lock, `repair:all` modules first, upgrade refused, sell price discount + offline fire, salvage cycle not profitable, test scenario) and the reworked light-repair assertion (below the 1000-Cr cushion); `Phase20Tests` +1 (deterministic panic decision, path-independent water test); `MiningAutopilotTests` +1 (Pip); `ThrottleTests` +7 cases (dead zone). Adjusted on purpose: `MiningAutopilotTests.NeverOverfillsTheCargo` (checks the hold at every moment: a raid can fail a cargo module and shrink the hold), `OutfitterShipyardTests` start-package list (+`test`), the Phase 24 hash test also reverts the repair price.

## Phase 27 (English summary): fleet combat — police and pirates through the order executors, squadrons, reinforcement, escort, pursuit, refuge escape, arrest and surrender; device-test fixes

Fifth stage of the combat plan (`docs/KAMPF.md` stage 27). Master switch `EconomyRules.FleetCombat` (default true; false = Phase 26 behaviour, bit-identical — `Phase27Tests.SchalterAus_…` reproduces the Phase 26 hash 8BAFCA8A395897AE, `RevertPhase27`).

**Police and pirates as commands (`Simulation/ForceCommands.cs`, `Simulation/Orders/ForceExecutors.cs`).** The faction/group decision only gives commands; the Phase 12 executors fly. `PoliceCommands.Step` (from `SecuritySystem`, interval 5): `ReturnCommand` when the ship needs its base (`NeedsBase`: hull < `RetreatHull`, ammo, fuel, failed module/fire), otherwise `PatrolCommand([DutySector])`; `PoliceCommands.Organize` (every `ManageTicks`, after `Assign`): police with the same duty sector form a squadron from `PoliceSquadronMin` (2) ships (owner = faction actor; a squadron stays together while members are serviced), and in territory sectors with threat ≥ `PoliceEscortThreat` (1.5) one patrol gives the most valuable laden economy-AI ship (`PoliceEscortMinValue` 1500) an `EscortCommand` while it stays in the territory (≤ `PoliceEscortTicks` 2400). `PirateCommands.Step` (from `PirateSystem`): rested raiders at home form a pack (squadron, ≤ `RaiderPackSize` 2) and get a `LurkCommand(sector, spot, until)` from `PirateSystem.PickHunt` (same draws as before); after the lurk window or a fight the pack returns (`ReturnCommand`), is serviced from the treasury and disbands at home. New executors (registered after the Phase 12 ones, `IOrderExecutor.ForPlayer` false — not in the fleet screen's command grid): `LurkExecutor` (fly to the spot, lurk window starts on arrival, rolls `TryIntercept`/`TryRaid` per tick; the raid chance of a pack is × the members in the sector) and `PursueExecutor` (`PursueCommand(target, lead, until)`: follows the target's destination, re-plans every 10 ticks; the lead shrinks in the same sector by (own − target speed) × seconds, or the own speed when the target is parked; at 0 → `Engage.StartIntercept`; if the target is already in a fight the pursuer joins it; ends at the border of `Jurisdiction` (own territory or allied ≥ `AllyRelation`), at the target's hideout, after `PursuitTicks` (1200) or when the target is gone; events `PursuitStarted/PursuitEnded`). `CombatSystem.Finish` → `PoliceCommands.AfterFight`: surviving police of a fight whose raider fled pursue it (start lead `PursuitLead` 40). `UnitSupport.Serve`: police/raiders at their own home base are serviced from the owner's treasury (`ServiceAtBase`) by `ReturnExecutor.Service`, and the emergency level sends them home instead of to the nearest repair station. `Engage.Resume` hands raiders to `PirateCommands.AfterFight`.

**Fighting together (`Combat/FleetCombat.cs`).** `Reinforcements.Step` (at the start of raids/intercepts/arrests and every `ReinforceTicks` 10): escorts/guards (`FleetGuards.Escorts`, now including police escorts) and combat-capable squadron/pack members of each active participant that are in the sector join its side (`JoinSide`, event `CombatJoined` with reason escort/guard/squadron/pack/pursuit/police). Tactic building blocks: `CombatSense.Focus` (weakest enemy, shield + hull, in own weapon range — focus fire for police, escorts and raiders without a victim) and `CombatSense.Attacker` (escorts first engage whoever targets their ward). All three are no-ops with the switch off.

**Refuge escape (`CombatRefuge`).** On `Engage.Join` every NPC participant remembers the nearest refuge within `CombatRefugeMaxDistance` (40): a station whose owner is not hostile to it (raiders: only their own hideout) or an open gate, unless an enemy stands closer to it (blocked). On the line each flee step shortens `CombatParticipant.Refuge`; in spatial fights the combat autopilot flies to `RefugePos`; reached (≤ `CombatRefugeRadius` 12 / 0) → dock at the station (`DockedAt` via `WasDockedAt`) or jump through the gate into the neighbour sector, leave as fled, event `RefugeReached`. Saved (`CombatParticipantSave.Refuge`) and hashed only when set.

**Player.** A wanted player (level ≥ 3) is warned and then attacked in a real fight (`EncounterCause.Arrest`, `Engage.StartArrest`, police of the sector owner up to a squadron; not while docked; offline — `PlayerRaids` off — the Phase 11 abstract interception remains). `Combat/PlayerSurrender`: `Issue`/`Surrender` before (pending warning) or during a fight — to police: `SecuritySystem.InterceptPlayer(…, faction)` (fine from the wallet, contraband seized, record cleared or reduced), the fight ends; to raiders: `PlayerSurrenderDropShare` (0.5) of every good is dropped, fenced into the pirate treasury (capped), the raiders turn home. Event `PlayerSurrendered`. Killing police in an arrest is assault. HUD button "Ergeben" (176×56, right column above "Dauerfeuer", `FlightHudData.CanSurrender`), alerts for arrest start/end and surrender.

**Display.** `Combat/FleetInfo` (pure): toasts for events in the player sector, `Links(u, sector)` (member → leader, escort → ward, pursuer → target) and `Describe`. Command-centre sector view draws the links (blue squadron, red pack, green escort, orange dashed pursuit), a "Flotten: …" line and a three-line legend. Fleet screen shows only `ForPlayer` executors.

**Review of Phase 26.** `Actor.CreatedTick` is hashed (the Phase 24 reference hash is now 68F0D589492ADEDC — hash extension only, determined on the unchanged Phase 26 code). Free extinguishing at the own base is kept on purpose (base = docking, docking extinguishes for everyone; comment in `NpcRepair.AtBase`). Station info and map labels see below.

**Device test (Samsung A15, APK Phase 26).** (1) Scrolling: Kit buttons accept the press, so `ScrollContainer`s never saw a drag, and command-centre/dock areas rebuild every tick (scroll position lost 4×/s). New `Ui/Kit/KitScroll` (replaces every `ScrollContainer`): reads touch/mouse in `_Input`, scrolls after a 12 px dead zone, cancels the button press below (`KitButton.CancelPress`); the screen bases rebuild via `SafeRebuild` (no rebuild during a drag, scroll positions captured/restored by tree order). Selftest stage 68 pushes a touch drag and a mouse drag over a "Starten" button. (2) Station info: fixed column widths, every row clips with "…", market rows short ("knapp/ok/Überschuss"), service chips with German names (`DockAreas.ServiceName`). (3) Outfitter: slot list scrolls, power/stat footer fixed inside the frame; `KitBar` is now a self-drawn control with exact height (the `ProgressBar` grew over the text below). (4) Planet: darker, far below and behind (camera `Far` 4000); credits and power mode live inside the status panel; 3D labels behind HUD elements are faded (`FlightHud.OccupiedRects`, `SectorView.Labels`). (5) Start packages scroll. (6) `ISessionScreen.ResetSession` on new game/load (sector view showed the old sector). (7) Repair price: the world file still set `repairPlatesPerHull` 0.05 — the Phase 26 correction (0.02) only changed the code default; the world file now says 0.02 (≈ 3 Cr per hull point at Werft Delta; test). (8) Hull loss after undocking at full thrust: reproduced in the selftest (undock at Wasserwerk Alpha, full thrust through the nearby gate into Nebelmark, then into a rock after the 1.5 s grace) — `Flight/UndockPath.Heading` picks a clear exit (radial, else turned in 20° steps; gates count as obstacles) for undocking and for the gate exit, grace after a jump 2.5 s; selftest stage 71. (9) Left-aligned buttons without symbol no longer reserve 58 px (texts were cut: "Befe…"), trade names fitted, taxi/universe card buttons, comms answers 72 px, hangar rental block inside the frame, "1 Einheit", power-mode message mentions overload only with a bank, save feedback in the system area. (10) Memory and splash: see weaknesses.

**Persistence.** Additive, save stays v17: commands `lurk`/`pursue` in `OrderSave`, `CombatParticipantSave.Refuge`, new rules; `UniverseHash` mixes the new commands, refuges (only when set) and `Actor.CreatedTick`. `EncounterCause.Arrest` is stored by name.

**Tests.** New `tests/SpaceSim.Tests/Phase27Tests.cs` (20): switch-off hash = Phase 26, police/pirates use commands, pack as squadron, police squadron per duty sector, return/service from the treasury, pack joins the raid + police focus fire, late escort joins and attacks its ward's attacker, refuge to station/through gate, blocked refuge and switch off, pursuit caught in the same sector / ended at the border, arrest + surrender, surrender during the warning and offline fallback, surrender to raiders (warning and fight), save/load bit-identical with commands/squadrons/refuge, `CreatedTick` hashed, determinism, repair price from the world file, clear undock heading at every station, 40000-tick runs (default and classic). Adjusted on purpose: `FleetCommandTests` (registry lists `lurk`/`pursue`; squadron test checks only the own squadron; v14 migration and "order layer changes nothing" run with the switch off — v14 had no commands and NPC forces now use the order layer; roundtrip counts only the player's squadrons), `SecurityTests` (three Phase 11 mechanics tests run with the switch off: abstract raid flow, smuggling/abstract interception, decommission without combat noise), `Phase24Tests.Ueberfall_Unbewaffneter…` (raider goes home by `ReturnCommand`), `Phase25Tests` hash test (reverts Phase 27, new constant for the hash extension), `RefuelRepairTests.Repair_Plattenmangel…` (expected points from the rule — the world file now says 0.02), `EconomyFixTests.Standardwelt_20000…` (copper smelter subsidy counted from tick 6000: 2600–4600 Cr fall in the start-up phase with the effective repair rule and fleet combat). No test removed.

**Balance (fresh Release build; before = HEAD Phase 26 via `git archive`).** All open runs "Preise im Zielbereich" (exit 0), the gate-17 run exit 2 only for the map split; every faction and pirate group viable (0 %). Default 20000 (twice, hash CB2A9FDBF34B15AB): loss rate 0.30 → 0.36 %, raids 62 (36 successful) → 68 (36), victims destroyed 2 → 0, raiders lost/built 0/0 → 1/1, pirate treasuries 8877/3662 → 9505/3591, police 5.4 → 5.3 % of treasury income, trader bankruptcies 1 → 0, trade/mine 26.5/22.1 → 26.4/22.1, HINWEIS 3 → 1, 0.36 → 0.37 ms/tick. 60000: 0.31 → 0.34 %, victims 3 → 0, bankruptcies 7 → 0. Seeds 1–8 at 20000: loss 0.34–0.61 → 0.26–0.50 % (mean 0.48 → 0.40), victims 8 → 3 in total, bankruptcies 3 → 1. 300000 default/seed 3/seed 4: loss 0.25/0.26/0.30 → 0.21/0.24/0.21 %, victims 5/7/6 → 2/3/3, raiders lost 11/11/5 → 4/3/3, **trader bankruptcies 7/10/0 → 0/2/7** (the Phase 26 "thin-load spiral" was mostly the ineffective repair rule — the world file still said 0.05), police 7.2–7.6 → 7.6–8.1 %, 0.154–0.162 → 0.163–0.166 ms/tick. Gate 17 (200000): 0.34 → 0.27 %, bankruptcies 8 → 3. Per 300000 ticks: 386–466 gate escapes, 39–63 station escapes, 155–171 pursuits (mostly ended at the border), 21–26 escorts, 30–72 packs. A first calibration with refuges up to 60 units cut successful raids from 49 % to 20 % and drained both pirate treasuries to 0 — the range is 40.

**Known weaknesses.** Police squadrons form and dissolve often (duty sectors follow the threat; 600–1000 formations per 300000 ticks, no cost). Pirate treasuries end long runs at 0–1000 Cr (costs > fence, as the Blades did before Phase 27); the Corsairs drop to one raider after ≈ 200000 ticks. Pursuits nearly always end at the border or the hideout (corsairs are faster than wardens) — pressure rather than catches. Police in an arrest never retreat because they are outnumbered; the player can surrender to police only while a patrol is in the fight. Splash time (~10 s on the device, probably .NET start plus the synchronous offline catch-up of up to 10000 ticks) and memory (785 MB PSS; no shadows/MSAA, glow left on) are documented, not changed. The player fleet has no "pursue/attack" command.

## Phase 28 (English summary): missiles, station defence and sieges, warships, war between factions; review points from Phase 27 and the second device test

Sixth stage of the combat plan (`docs/KAMPF.md` stage 28). Master switch `EconomyRules.Warfare` (default true; false + removing the new content = Phase 27 bit-identical: `Phase28Tests.SchalterAus_…` reproduces CB2A9FDBF34B15AB with `RevertPhase28`, which also resets `arrestCooldownTicks`/`surrenderFineAsDebt`). In the default world no war can break out (relations +0.12…+0.25, declaration threshold −0.6); the mechanics are proven by tests and a hostile test world.

**Missiles (`Combat/Missiles.cs`).** A weapon module with stat `missileSpeed` > 0 is a launcher (`Missiles.IsMissile`); it uses the third damage type `explosive` (`DamageType.Explosive`, rules `ExplosiveVsShield` 0.8, `ExplosiveVsArmor` 1.2) and consumes one missile from the separate missile magazine (`ShipStats.MissileCapacity` = Σ `missileCapacity`, runtime deficit `ShipSystemsState.MissilesMissing`, saved/hashed only when ≠ 0; with `ammoActive` off magazines count as full). Firing (NPC `CombatSystem.Fire`, player `PlayerGunnery.TryFire`, station guns) starts a `MissileFlight` in the encounter (`Encounter.Missiles`, saved/hashed only when present) that flies distance ÷ speed without a random draw; on arrival the target's **point defence** (`ShipStats.PointDefense` = Σ `pointDefense`, station `defense.pointDefense`, capped by `PointDefenseMax` 0.8) draws one number, then the shared `Hit.Roll` (two draws) with the target's speed at that moment (**evasion**). A target that left the fight, or an encounter that ends, loses its missiles ("lost", no draw). Events `MissileLaunched`, `MissileResolved`. Content: good `missile` "Rakete" (category `missile`, 45 Cr, recipe in the weapons works 2 steel + 1 electronics + 1 fuel cell → 6 per 40 ticks, shelves at yards and the police station [2,6]), modules `missile_launcher_s` (weapon S, damage 30, reload 8, range 110, tracking 0.75, speed 35, magazine 6) and `point_defense_s` (utility, 0.35). Rearm: `rearm:missiles` offer (only ships with a launcher), NPC service units at home (`Missiles.AtHome` in `ServiceAtBase`).

**Station defence and attack (`Combat/StationCombat.cs`, `Combat/Siege.cs`).** `stationTypes[].defense { hull, shield, shieldRegen, armor, pointDefense, guns[] }` (validated; HQ, sector HQ, police station, shipyard), other stations have `StationBaseHull` 800 and no guns. Runtime `Station.Condition` (`StationCondition`: hull/shield deficits, shield pause, gun timers, disabled; null = intact; saved/hashed only when set). In every encounter the guns of stations in the sector fire at hostile active participants in range (`StationCombat.Hostile`: attackers of a besieged station, enemies at war, actors with hostile relation such as outlaws, a player wanted ≥ 3 or attacking the station's faction) — the station feeds its guns itself. **Sieges** (`EncounterCause.Siege`, `Encounter.Station` and its line position `StationX`): warships with `AssaultCommand` (`AssaultExecutor`, not a player command) or the player (`PlayerGunnery.TryFireAtStation`, heavy crime `assault_station`) attack; police and warships of the station's faction in the sector defend (`Siege.Reinforce`); the station itself keeps the defending side alive (`StationCombat.Defends`). New tactic `siege` (enemy ships in reach first, otherwise the station). At hull 0 the station falls (`StationFell`): **captured** only with `allowStationCapture` and `StationProtection.IsCapturable` (AI attackers capture for their faction, the player for himself), **destroyed** through `Departures` (reason a, detail `siege`) where `IsDestructible` and not a government station, otherwise **disabled** (guns silent, no war target) until repaired. `StationRepairSystem` (Combat module, interval 50): shield regenerates, hull up to `StationRepairPerDay` 600 per day outside a siege using `StationRepairPlatesPerHull` 0.02 hull plates from the own stock, the rest at base × 1.5 from the owner faction's treasury (`TreasuryUpkeep` "station_repair") — the material sink; disabled stations come back at 25 %. The player's escape rule now also counts his own reach and a besieged station's guns (with missiles a long-range shot used to end the fight at once).

**Warships (`Simulation/Wars.cs` `NavySystem`, Security module, interval 100).** Class `frigate` "Fregatte" (L, heavy kit, 14 slots: two reactors, two bow and two turret weapons, two shields, two armour plates, magazine, point defence, power bank) and model `bastion` "Bollwerk" (2 launchers, laser, mass driver; no jobs, sold at yards — also to the player). Role `ShipRole.Warship`; service unit (serviced and rearmed at its home base from the treasury). Only at war a faction buys up to `WarshipsPerWar` 2 per war (max `MaxWarshipsPerFaction` 3) through `ShipPurchase` from `FactionViability.Spendable` (never below the reserve), pays `WarshipUpkeepPerTick` 1.5 ("navy"), sends them against the nearest enemy station (`Wars.PickTarget`) and decommissions them `WarshipPeaceTicks` 6000 after peace or at once when the treasury falls below the reserve (`Departures` reason c). Warships route with the physical profile (they break through locks).

**War (`Wars`, `WarSystem`, Politics module, interval 100).** `Universe.Wars` (`War`: A, B, since, weariness, losses, sieges) and `Universe.PeaceSince` (saved, hashed when present). Declaration (`Wars.DeclareIssue`): both State/Guild, no running war, no peace within `PeaceCooldownTicks` 30000, relation ≤ `WarDeclareRelation` −0.6 shifted by aggression, war chest `Spendable` ≥ `WarChest` 15000, strength (police + warships + spendable money ÷ 20000) ≥ 0.6 × enemy; at most one declaration per step; `WarDeclarations` switch. During war the relation stays ≤ `WarRelation` −0.8 (Phase 10 locks follow by themselves). Weariness = duration ÷ `WarWearyTicks` 40000 + losses ÷ `WarLossScale`; peace at weariness 1 after `WarMinTicks` 6000, or at once when a treasury falls below its reserve ("broke") — no war decision can make a faction unable to act; relations are set to `PeaceRelation` −0.2. Events `WarDeclared`, `PeaceConcluded`, `SiegeStarted`, `WarshipCommissioned/Decommissioned`.

**Review points from Phase 27.** (a) `Actor.ArrestCooldownUntil` (`ArrestCooldownTicks` 2400): after escaping a warning, an arrest fight or surrendering no new arrest warning for that time (no loop). (b) `SurrenderFineAsDebt`: surrendering to the police collects the full fine — the wallet may go negative — and clears the record (before: an empty wallet lowered the level without payment). (c) Seed 4/300000 compared with `--fleetcombat off` on the Phase 27 code: trader bankruptcies 7 vs 2, police destroyed 9 vs 3, raiders destroyed 3 vs 12 — fleet combat moves the losses from pirates to police (packs, pursuits) at the same police cost (8.1 vs 7.9 % of treasury income); the bankruptcies show no combat link (thin-load spiral, path dependent); no new rule. (d) Outfitter hint/footer shortened, sector legend three lines, the large alert is smaller and sits below the ship (selftest checks no overlap). (e) Pirate treasuries: with fleet combat raids succeed less often (refuge escapes), fence income (111k) fell below costs (121k: upkeep/rebuilds/repairs 104k, ammo 17k per 300000 ticks) — default world `fenceShare` 0.6 → 0.75 (does not change the loss rate); both groups keep two raiders and end long runs at 4 000–9 700 Cr. (f) `OfflineCatchUp.Begin` → `CatchUpJob` (same ticks and preparations as `Run`, `Finish` restores the rule also on abort); the view runs it over several frames with a progress overlay (`Hud.ShowLoading`) instead of synchronously in `_Ready`; pausing mid catch-up drops the rest and saves.

**Second device test (Phase 27 APK).** (1) Scroll positions are restored by a stable key (`KitScroll.ScrollKey` = file:line of its creation) instead of tree order, and no rebuild happens while a finger rests on a list (`KitScroll.Dragging` also counts a held finger) — selftest stage 78. (2) Outfitter shows the offer's reason ("erst reparieren") before "Nicht genügend Credits". (3) Docked foreign ships are no target (bay state `NoTarget`, "Ziel angedockt") and never offered by "Nächster Gegner". (4) Time-lapse + autopilot over two jumps: the sector chip followed in the headless selftest (stage 77) — not reproduced. (5) Shipyard rows say "Bergbaulaser"/weapons count; "Route planen" and the sector autopilot return to the flight view with the toast; the sector view header names the selected sector; the hamburger "Neues Spiel" opens the start-package chooser; trade "Max" checked (rule = floor of stock, money, hold) — not reproduced. (6) Start packages `combat` "Gefechtstest (Test)" (Falcon with cargo 130 from Werft Delta, a raider pack of two in weapon range announcing a raid after 6 s, a free trader, a police squadron) and `arrest` "Festnahmetest (Test)" (wanted level 3, police squadron — warning within seconds), data-driven via new `ScenarioDef` fields (`distance`, `raiders`, `raidAfterTicks`, `trader`, `policeSquadron`, `wanted`).

**UI.** Flight: "Station angreifen" (two taps, `KitConfirm`) for a selected station when allowed; the weapons then fire at the station (target card with `WarInfo.StationLine`), station shots and shots at stations as beams, missiles as glowing spheres (`CombatView.UpdateMissiles` from Core data), alerts for war/peace/fallen stations. Station info rows "Verteidigung"/"Geschütze"; factions detail shows "Frieden"/"Krieg mit …". Selftest stages 73–78; `--ui-shots … --ui-shots-phase28` adds 78a, 78–82.

**Persistence.** Additive (save stays v17): `StationTypeSave.Defense`, `StationSave.Condition`, `EncounterSave.Station/StationX/Missiles`, `ShipSystemsSave.MissilesMissing`, `ActorSave.ArrestCooldownUntil`, `SaveGame.Wars/PeaceSince`, order kind `assault`; `SaveGameJson.AfterV17Keys` += missile, launcher, point defence, frigate, bastion; the migration base drops `defense`.

**Tests.** New `Phase28Tests` (31 test methods, 34 cases incl. the theory); 761 tests green, selftest OK. Adjusted on purpose: module/class/model counts, `FirstModuleGoodId` 28 (`DemoUniverse.Missile` 27), JSON goods 27, ammo no longer the last JSON good, missiles exempt from "used somewhere" like ammo, propellant test allows `missile`, registry lists `assault`, Phase 24/26 hash tests also revert Phase 28, start-package list, `Phase27Tests.Speichern_…` places the trader within refuge range (its precondition relied on a random police join). No test removed.

**Balance (fresh Release build; before = Phase 27).** All open runs "Preise im Zielbereich", gate run exit 2 only for the split; no war, no warship, no siege in any run. Default 20000 twice hash 0FC4EDCC51E1D4D6: loss 0.36 → 0.34 %, raids 68 → 59, HINWEIS 1 → 1, 0.37 → 0.39 ms/tick. 60000: 0.34 → 0.45 %, bankruptcies 0 → 5. Seeds 1–8 at 20000: loss 0.17–0.46 % (Phase 27 0.26–0.50), bankruptcies 1 in total. 300000 default/seed 3/seed 4: loss 0.21/0.24/0.21 → 0.35/0.28/0.37 %, trader bankruptcies 0/2/7 → 16/2/9, police destroyed 1/–/9 → 6/4/1, raiders destroyed 4/3/3 → 11/9/9, pirate treasuries at the end 0–1 000 → 4 000–9 700 with two raiders each, police 7.1–7.3 % of treasury income, 0.165 → 0.164–0.173 ms/tick. Gate 17 (200000): loss 0.27 → 0.44 %, bankruptcies 3 → 4. Station guns fire 92 (20000) to 550 (300000) shots per run, missiles (HQ launchers) 2–171.

**Known weaknesses.** Trader bankruptcies in long runs rose on the default seed (0 → 16; 60000: 0 → 5); with `--warfare off` (content kept) it was 23, without the missile good 1 at 60000 — the content shift changes the path into the known thin-load spiral, a clean rule was not found. Station guns kill more raiders near stations (raiders destroyed 4 → 11 per 300000). The player cannot capture stations in the default world (capture off); government crisis after losing an HQ, tribute, alliances, mediation and NPC smaller "feuds" are not implemented; wars between AI factions only start in custom/hostile worlds. Warships do not escort or fight enemy warships outside sieges. Device-test points 4 and "Max" could not be reproduced.

**Correction after review (Phase 28).**
- *Trader bankruptcies (cause found with the new balance probe `--probe pleite`, diagnostic event `TraderBought`):* a trader arrived at its seller after the price there had risen above the buyer's bid and bought anyway (e.g. 44 energy for 588 Cr, sold for 171) — the start of the thin-load spiral of Phases 20/22/26. Rule `TraderBuyGuard` (default true) / `TraderBuyGuardShare` (1.0) in `TraderSystem` (GoingToBuy): an AI trader does not buy when seller price ≥ buyer price × share; the order is dropped, event `TraderBuyRefused`, balance line "Händler-Einkäufe mit sicherem Verlust verweigert". 300000 ticks default/seeds 1–4: bankruptcies 16/2/2/2/9 → 0/0/0/0/0, unprofitable 0.
- *Last producer destroyed:* rule `BuildMissingProducer` (true) / `MissingProducerRatio` (5) in `StationLifecycleSystem.Scan`: a recipe-consumed good with no finished producer and no producing site counts as scarce when some station type can make it (ores excluded through the producer check).
- *Pirates:* default world `raiderUpkeepPerTick` 0.03 (code default unchanged); treasuries end long runs at 0–9300 Cr, mostly two raiders per group.
- *Debts:* every buy/service path checks the wallet (`Schulden_KeinKaufOderDienstMitNegativerKasse`); berth fee, rental rent, fleet upkeep, emergency repair and the surrender fine run into debt by design. `StationRepairSystem` looks up hull plates once per step.
- *UI/device test:* short station target card ("Hülle · Schild", `WarInfo.StationShort`), station info defence/profitability on several rows, war line in factions, hit warning with a backdrop below the toast area, `Hud.Alert(text, seconds, sticky)` (alert and toast no longer overlap, sticky alerts above dock screens, used for the loss message, which names rental fee/deposit/rent); "Ergeben" visible whenever `PlayerSurrender.Relevant`, disabled with the reason ("Keine Fracht zum Abwerfen."); autopilot circling at HQ Alpha fixed by `PathFollower` (Navigator.cs: plans once, ticks waypoints off within `Reach` 4, sidesteps and replans after `StallCalls` 25; `PathPlanner.Plan(from, to, obs, margin)` with margin + ship radius for the player only, NPC travel unchanged); tapping a station only selects it; start-package cards sized by text, delayed scroll restore, menu system entries two per row; scenario `combat` reworked (`ScenarioDef.RaiderHull`, lead raider ahead of the nose, raid after 15 s) and new start option `war` "Kriegstest (Test)". Selftest stages 79 (autopilot from four starts) and 80 (surrender button).
- *Tests:* `RevertPhase28` also resets fence share, raider upkeep, `TraderBuyGuard`, `BuildMissingProducer`; `MultiroleTests.Tinker_…` accepts a refused loss buy; `ExportPlanetTests.Haendler_KauftNurWasDerAbnehmerBezahlenKann` switches the guard off (it tests the payment rule); start-package lists. New `Phase28Tests`: loss-buy refusal, debts, last producer rebuilt, war test package, autopilot reaches every station from six starts. 766 tests green, selftest OK.
- *Balance (before = Phase 28):* default 20000 twice hash DE000885191EF812, loss 0.34 → 0.21 %; 60000 0.45 → 0.28 %, bankruptcies 5 → 0; seeds 1–4 at 20000 0.26–0.36 %, 0 bankruptcies; 300000 default/seeds 1–4 loss 0.18–0.25 %, bankruptcies 0, HINWEIS 0, 0.173–0.187 ms/tick; gate 17 (200000) 0.44 → 0.25 %. All runs "Preise im Zielbereich".
- *Open:* a laser-cannon HINWEIS appears only in 20000-tick runs (early phase, already in Phase 27; the single weapons works waits for inputs 71–86 % of the time — a second one did not help clearly); pirate treasuries on seeds 1/3 end at 0; the Falke without a hold cannot surrender to raiders; the device points were not checked on a device.

**Second device test (Phase 28 follow-up APK).** UI and operation only; the simulation is unchanged (default 20000 ticks still hash DE000885191EF812, checked with a Debug run of the runner).
- *Important messages:* `Hud.Alert` lives on its own `CanvasLayer` (layer 100) above every screen, catches the tap itself (mouse filter Stop + `AcceptEvent`; before, it sat in the tree before the dock screens and the tap went to the tile below), has a 56 px "✕" button, a maximum duration of 20 s (`Hud.AlertMaxSeconds`, also for sticky alerts), sits top centre (600 × 78, at most three lines; in flight under the sector chip, over full screens in their header), is cleared by `AttachSession` (new game/load) and a normal alert never replaces a sticky one (it becomes a toast). The loss message (`ShipLoss.LoseActive(…, cause)`) always names cause (pirates/police/station guns/impact), landing station, new ship (own/rental/emergency rental) and the amounts (fee + deposit, daily rent, forfeited deposit and open rent of a lost rental).
- *Trade quantity:* the list editor is created in `TradeRow._Ready`, after `SetQty` had been called — the chosen quantity was lost on every rebuild (four per second): "Max" showed and bought 1, "+" seemed to work every other time. The row now remembers the quantity. Screens no longer rebuild while a Kit button is held (`KitButton.AnyHeld`, set of pressed buttons, 8 s safety). `KitScroll` applies a remembered position right after layout (`SortChildren`), so lists no longer flash to the top; `TradeScreen` lost its own scroll handling and scrolls an opened row fully into view. The start-package list parses the default world once (it was parsed on every rebuild).
- *Combat test scenario:* new `ScenarioDef` fields `bonusSlots` (extra slot types, size S), `leadWithoutShield`, `reinforceAfterTicks`, `reinforceHull`. The Falke gets a cargo slot with food/ammo; the lead raider sits ahead without shield at 40 % hull and with rules of engagement "Angriff" — a raider with `Engagement.Attack` does not flee (`RaiderTactic`; NPC raiders never have it); the second raider lurks far away and raids 60 s after the first raid (`PirateSystem.ResolvePending`: a raid that comes due while the player is already fighting joins that fight on the raider side instead of being resolved abstractly). The view faces the announced raider at session start and shows the warning for the full 15 s (`Main.AnnouncePending`; the heading of the previous session was kept before). Core test: firing only at the next enemy keeps the Falke alive > 45 s and kills the lead (bounty).
- *Small fixes:* surrender reason "Keine Fracht"; sector card without the sector name, universe card "· du hier"; start-package cards and `UiKit.WrappedText` measure text with the font; a selected station with defence values shows hull/shield on the target card before an attack; the Nomad (no mining laser, no weapon) shows no "Laser" bay (`WeaponBays.For(u, ship)`); station info "Allgemein" card scrolls instead of growing out of its frame; autopilot path follower resets on a new target or when switched on again, and arriving at a station says "Angedockt per Autopilot"; war-test description names Werft/Polizeiwache Vesta for missile rearming.
- *Tests:* `Phase28Tests` combat-scenario test rewritten (two raid announcements, lead ahead outside its range, 40 % hull without shield, reinforcement ≥ 60 s later, cargo → surrender enabled), new `Startpaket_Gefechtstest_BestehbarMitDauerfeuer`. Selftest stages 81 (alert over the dock menu: a simulated tap closes it and triggers nothing below, "✕", no alert after "new game", info card, Nomad bays), 82 (trade "Max"/"−"/"+"/"Kaufen" by simulated taps while ticks and rebuild attempts run between press and release; opened last row fully visible, scroll position held), 83 (combat test passed with "Nächster Gegner" + "Dauerfeuer"). Stage 76 checks the new alert position. UI shots 83–89.

**Long-run review (Phase 28 final long test, `/home/gerd/space-adventure-langlaeufe/phase28`).**
- *Pirates died out in long runs (A/B/C/F/J/K).* Cause (balance probe `--probe piraten`: state of every raider each 20000 ticks): once a pirate treasury was empty, a damaged raider (hull below `EmergencyHullFraction` or a failed module) sat at its hideout in an endless loop — emergency level → fly home → `ServiceAtBase` repairs nothing without money → emergency ends → the lurk command restarts → emergency again. With a single raider per group and no money for a rebuild, the group never raided again (A: 0 raids from tick 740 000 on).
