Rule `PirateBasePatchHull` (0.6, 0 = off) / `PirateBasePatchPerTick` (0.0004): a raider in its own hideout (not fighting) is patched for free and slowly up to 60 % hull, failed modules up to just above the failure limit, fires out (`PirateSystem.PatchAtBase`, called from the pirate booking every 50 ticks); paid full repair stays `NpcRepair.AtBase`. New viability issue `CannotHunt` for outlaw groups (`FactionViability.CannotHunt`: no raider is operational or being serviced in the hideout, and no rebuild is possible); the balance tool counts it in "Handlungsfähigkeit" (0–0.3 % of the samples in long runs, the patching time). Result 1 036 800 ticks: default raids per 20 000 ticks 25–59, never 0 (before 0 from t740k), loss rate 0.21 % (before 0.13 %); seed 3 24–51 (before 11–19 at the end), 0.17 % (0.12 %); J (seed 3, alternating closures, 2 073 600 ticks): never 0 (8–53), 0.13 % (0.05 %).
- *Mining above the limit after reunification (run J: `mine 25.9/22`, max 29.5, 24 miners from tick 1 000 000).* Cause: while split, each part buys up to its share of the limit, but ships stuck in a small part sit above that part's share, so the sum exceeded the world limit; after reunification the surplus stayed (it is profitable, so reason c never applies). Purchase-side fix `FleetWorldCapWhenDivided` (true): while divided, normal purchases (`FleetSystem.CanGrow`) also require the world total to be below the limit; only the faction's rescue purchase for a part whose fleet collapsed (`BuyFromTreasury`) may exceed it. J now ends at `mine 22.5/22 (max 22.5)`, 20 miners.
- *Tests:* `Phase28Tests.Piraten_LeereKasse_BeschaedigtImVersteck_JagenWieder` (theory: with the rule the broke group hunts again within 6000 ticks, without it `CannotHunt`), `Flotte_GeteilteKarte_NieUeberDieWeltgrenze`; `RevertPhase28` also switches both rules off. Open runs up to 300 000 ticks are bit-identical to before (default 20 000 hash DE000885191EF812, 60 000 739E84C99C115D87, 300 000 A6DD919B855E029D — the patch only acts once a group is broke and damaged); gate 17 (200 000) changed (hash DDCE1B53B5150281, loss 0.25 → 0.30 %).

**Review round 2 follow-up (Phase 28).**
- *Pending raid while the player fights:* `PirateSystem.ResolvePending` lets the raider join a running fight of the player only when the player's opposite side already has raiders; in an arrest or an assault by the player the raid is postponed (new deadline `PlayerWarningTicks`, comes after the fight), never resolved abstractly and never on the police/victim side. Test `Ueberfall_WaehrendGefecht_TrittNurGegenKapererBei` (theory).
- *Hideout patch only when broke:* `PirateSystem.PatchAtBase` acts only when the owner treasury cannot pay the full base repair (`NpcRepair.BaseCost`). Default 20 000 ticks with `--planguard off` still hash DE000885191EF812; 1 036 800 ticks: raids per 20 000 ticks 33–55, `CannotHunt` 0 %.
- *Trader empty runs:* rule `TraderPlanGuard` (true): `TraderSystem.BestTrade` already skips a pair when the expected seller price at arrival — today's price scaled by the market formula factor target ÷ stock after the purchases other traders already plan there (`StockFactor`) — reaches the buyer price × `TraderBuyGuardShare`. Measured variants at 60 000 ticks: seller and buyer side (incoming deliveries) together cut refusals 2917 → 628 but lowered trade revenue −3.5 % and exports −8 % (300 000: CG −20 %); the seller side alone: refusals 2917 → 1210, revenue +2.6 %, exports up — adopted. Balance tool `--planguard on|off`. Test `Haendler_PlanenMitErwartetemEinkaufspreis_WenigerLeerfahrten`.
- *UI:* the alert sits at y 84 in flight (560 wide, clear of the situation line and the target card; toasts below it are 416 wide, left of the combat buttons) and in the header between area title and credits on full screens and the dock menu (600 × 56, two lines, `ScreenAlertX` 420 in the 1280 grid); station info "Angebotene Dienste" moved into the "Politik & Hinweise" card (both cards scroll if needed, the selftest requires that nothing is cut off at Sektor-HQ Beta); "Nächster Gegner" of an armed ship without an enemy in the sector is disabled with "kein Gegner".
- *Balance (before = round 2):* default 20 000 twice hash 506C31B3B6B6B902, refusals 702 → 267, loss 0.21 → 0.33 %, revenue +1.9 %; 60 000 refusals 2917 → 1210, revenue +2.6 %, exports food/CG/luxury +1.5/+3.3/+8.7 %; seeds 1–4 refusals 624/583/554/519 → 211/120/242/348, revenue +3.8/+1.1/+3.1/−0.2 %, loss 0.52/0.31/0.30/0.25 % (before 0.26/0.34/0.29/0.36 %); 300 000 refusals 13 051 → 5915, revenue +3.0 %, exports +1.8/+2.0/+4.6 %, loss 0.27 %; gate 17 refusals 7464 → 4077, revenue +3.1 %, food export −4.6 %; 1 036 800 refusals 46 645 → 22 135, revenue +1.8 %, loss 0.23 %. All "Preise im Zielbereich", no trader bankruptcies.

**Third device test (APK 115ba8b).**
- *Aim assist:* `Flight/AimAssist` (pure: `Turn`, `Bearing`, `Delta`, `PlayerTurnRate` 3.2 rad/s = the view's turn speed). HUD toggle "Ziel folgen" (764, 330; visible with a hostile ship target or an armed station; on by default, "Dauerfeuer" switches it on): without a turn button, autopilot or auto-mining the view turns the nose towards the target at the ship's turn rate; ◀ ▶ override. Measured: the raider in the combat test circles the Falke at ≤ 0.16 rad/s, the turn rate is 3.2 rad/s — the problem was operation, not rules. A raid on the player selects the attacker as target and the alert says so ("Ziel gewählt … „Dauerfeuer“ an?"). The core test "bestehbar" now uses `AimAssist.Turn` with the real turn rate instead of ideal facing; selftest stage 84 passes the combat test with "Dauerfeuer" + aim assist only.
- *Station attack:* the button shows the defence (`WarInfo.DefenseWarning`: "2 Geschütze · 60 m · Raketen 110 m"), the missile bay its stock ("Raketen 6/6"), missiles are drawn larger with a trail. Core fix: in a siege the player counted as fled when the distance to the station *centre* exceeded his reach although `TryFireAtStation` measures to the hull (`Station.BodyRadius`) — firing from the edge of missile range ended the siege in the next tick and the missile vanished; the escape check now subtracts the body radius (sieges only, default-world hash unchanged). War test description names the gun range. Test `Belagerung_RaketeAmRandDerReichweite_KommtAn`, selftest stage 85.
- *Stations off screen:* small arrows with names at the edge of the free centre field (`Hud.StationScreenPositions`, `Hud.EdgeMarkers`); tapping one selects the station. Selftest stage 86.
- *Small fixes:* empty wrecks (only scrap below `SalvageMinCondition`) are no longer drawn; the rental return message names the whole rent incl. the days already charged daily (the 120 Cr had been charged by `ChargeRent` before the return); over the dock menu the alert sits left of the window (multi-line, the title stays free); toasts live on the alert layer (they were hidden under the backdrop of open menus, e.g. the save confirmation); docking at another station resets the trade editor and its old message.
- *Memory:* selftest stage 87 runs two rounds of 200 trade rebuilds + 5 sector changes: nodes 1770 → 1773, objects 5442 → 5449, orphans 0, resources 39, managed heap ≈ 12 MB, static memory +1 MB in the second round (+17 MB in the first, one-off allocations). No leak in the view's node/object counts; the PSS growth on the device (713 MB → 1.02 GB) is not reproduced here.

**Fourth device test (APK 0708228).** UI only; default-world hash unchanged (506C31B3B6B6B902).
- *Ranges:* measured with a probe — the hits at 81–94 m during the station attack came from police ships that joined the siege, not from the station guns (centre distance ≤ 60 m as their rule says). Display is now consistent in centre distance: target card "Entfernung" (centre), the button sub "2 Geschütze · 60 m" (`WarInfo.DefenseWarning`), and a status line on the target card (`WarInfo.RangeStatus`: "IN GESCHÜTZREICHWEITE (60 m)" / "außerhalb Geschütze · Raketen ja" / "… Raketen ≤125 m"; own missile reach from the centre = weapon range + `Station.BodyRadius`, `WarInfo.MissileReachFromCentre`). The hit warning names the shooter ("BESCHUSS (Polizei)! ▲ von vorn"). War test starts at 80 m from the dock point (≈ 102 m from the centre: outside the guns, inside missile reach) — asserted in `Startpaket_Kriegstest…`.
- *Station attack:* arming switches "Dauerfeuer" and "Ziel folgen" on (the toast said the weapons fire, but nothing fired without "Dauerfeuer"). A deliberate "Ziel folgen" off is remembered for that target (`_aimOffFor`), "Dauerfeuer"/attack do not override it until the target changes.
- *Stations:* edge arrows ordered by defence first, then distance (max 10; the HQ had been hidden behind a nearer marker), names and arrows never over HUD rects; tapping a station body in screen space (projected radius, ≥ 28 device px) wins over ships; command centre → sector: "Ziel wählen" next to "Autopilot hin".
- *Small:* 3D labels hidden when any part (not only the centre) lies under the HUD; NPC raid toast names the ship ("Hier im Sektor: Überfall auf Schiff 12 abgewehrt …"); toasts on full screens bottom centre, over the dock menu left of the window, in flight at y 122; dock menu footer on two lines; save message spaced; new game resets the time lapse, aim assist and auto-fire; rental return "Miete 120 Cr (bereits abgebucht), Pfand 400 Cr zurück" incl. the unpaid case; bounty messages name the nearest office of the faction (`CourtService.NearestOffice/Hint`); the combat test lead carries 6 luxury goods so its wreck has cargo for the tractor (a wreck without salvageable modules or cargo is intentionally not drawn). The dock fuel bar only appears with `fuelActive` (the UI-shot world has fuel on).

**Fifth device test (user decision: attacking a station needs a frigate or a fleet, not a lone fighter).** Default-world hash unchanged (506C31B3B6B6B902, twice).
- *War test start package:* `war` now starts in the frigate `bastion` with an own squadron of two more Bollwerke (new `ScenarioDef.Wingmen`, `StartScenario.ApplyWingmen`: player-owned ships next to the player, squadron with the active ship as leader, rules "Angriff", members follow). Squadron helpers that join a siege on the attacker side get the `siege` tactic (`Reinforcements.JoinSide`; the escort tactic held still without an enemy ship); a player wingman below `EscortRetreatHull` does not rejoin at once. Core test `Startpaket_Kriegstest_MitVerbandSchaffbar`: approaching to 40 m with aim assist and auto-fire disables Sektor-HQ Beta incl. its police in ≈ 250 s, the Bollwerk survives; alone it is lost (≈ 160 s).
- *Station shield (cause of "missiles barely scratch the shield"):* a hit on the hull did not pause the shield, so an empty shield recharged mid-fire, and `StationRepairSystem` (interval 50) recharged a whole step right after the pause. Now every hit pauses the shield and only the time since the pause ended recharges.
- *Odds warning:* `WarInfo.SiegeOdds/SiegeOddsText/SiegeHopeless` (pure): own side = active ship + `Reinforcements.Helpers(u, sector, ship)`; defence = stations of the faction in the sector (guns as weapon power, shield ÷ 20, hull ÷ 50, armour ÷ 5) + police/warships there. Below 1.2 "Aussichtslos allein – Fregatte oder Flotte nötig" (or "Verband zu schwach (a : b)"), below 1.6 "Knapp", else "Überlegen". Shown on the target card (new line) and as button sub "Aussichtslos!"; the attack toast repeats it. A hint, no ban.
- *Loss message names the last shooter:* `Engage.Destroy(…, by)`; `Gunnery.Shoot` passes `CombatSense.Who` (Polizei/Piraten/Kriegsschiff/owner), station guns "Stationsgeschütze (name)", missiles "Rakete: …"/"Stationsrakete (…)".
- *Siege escape:* the escape rule is one pure function `CombatSense.EscapeBlock` (used by `CombatSystem` and the HUD line "Flucht: Polizei in Reichweite (52 von 60 m)", `PlayerCombat.EscapeStatus`). Cause of "211 m and still fighting": the player's own weapon reach (missiles 110 m) kept every pursuing patrol in the fight; now it counts only while the player is still shooting (`CombatSense.StillShooting`: a reload timer running or an own missile in flight). Station guns are measured from the station centre like their firing rule (before: to the hull).
- *Target card:* "Ziel wählen" / selecting a station or autopilot target clears a selected ship and refreshes the card at once. Tapping: a ship next to a station wins when the tap is closer to its marker than to the station centre (station picking radius ≥ 28 device px, ship ≥ 56).
- Tests: `Phase28Tests` +3 (war test with squadron, alone hopeless + last shooter in the loss message, escape rule incl. display text); `RevertPhase28` also drops start options of the bastion. Selftest stage 85 checks the squadron, odds on the card ("Überlegen" with, "Aussichtslos allein" without wingmen) and the escape line; stage 86 checks that "Ziel wählen" replaces a selected ship on the card.
- Open: station guns measure from the centre while ship weapons measure to the hull (a ship at 62–82 m from the centre hits with lasers/missiles outside the guns); the odds are a strength estimate (with two police present the winning three-frigate squadron reads "Aussichtslos – Verband zu schwach").

**Sixth device test (station fell in ≈ 25 s instead of minutes).** Cause (reproduced in a Core probe: fire once, let the player count as fled while the wingmen keep the siege running, then fire every frame like auto-fire): the player's next shot found his old inactive participant (`Encounter.Of` returns the first entry), did not rejoin, and `ReloadLeft` read no encounter (0) — 700 shots in 10 s, station 2003 → 260 hull+shield, and the player was no target for guns and defenders. He had left in the first place because his reload timer ran to 0 in the same tick as the escape check (between two salvos). Fixes: `Engage.Rejoin` reactivates the old entry (no second entry, no random draw) for the active player (`TryFireAtStation`, `PlayerCombat.Engage`) and player-owned helpers (`Reinforcements.JoinSide`); `CombatParticipant.LastShotTick` (saved/hashed only when set) keeps the own weapon reach in the escape rule for `PlayerFireHoldTicks` (40 = 10 s) after the last own shot; a siege with the active player is exempt from `CombatMaxTicks` (it used to end after 60 s and restart with the next shot, sending the police away). Same probe after the fix: 13 shots in 10 s. Selftest stage 88 drives the real view path (new:war, "Station angreifen", 50 % thrust, 20 s real time at ×1): 15 HP/s against 17.7 HP/s in the Core over the same 20 s.
- *Siege length:* with the timeout gone the police stayed and the squadron needed ≈ 400 s; besides, crippled police re-joined every 10 ticks (`Siege.Reinforce` now skips defenders below their retreat hull and reuses an old entry). Sector HQ hull 1800 → 1300: the squadron disables it in ≈ 190 s (test asserts 2–4 min), alone the Bollwerk is still lost. NPC ships that rejoin an encounter elsewhere still get a second entry (changing that changed the default-world hash; left as is).
- *Retreat:* `Siege.Withdraw` — when the active player leaves his siege as fled (escape rule, docking, jump) his own ships in it leave too and resume their command (squadron members follow the leader); with no attacker left the siege ends the same tick. Event `SiegeWithdrawn` ("Rückzug: Angriff auf … abgebrochen — dein Verband (2) folgt dir."); the view disarms the station attack and auto-fire and shows siege ends. A dropped arrest warning names its reason (already fighting, docked, no patrol, wanted level fell) instead of always "Du hast dich der Kontrolle entzogen".
- Station missiles carry `MissileFlight.FromStation` (saved, not hashed — label only) instead of being recognised by shooter id 0. Tests: `Phase28Tests.Belagerung_RueckzugBeendetSie_WiedereinstiegMitNachladeuhr`, escape and war tests extended. Default-world hash unchanged (506C31B3B6B6B902).

**Seventh round: a ship never has two entries in one encounter.** Rule `CombatRejoinReuse` (default true; `RevertPhase28` switches it off, balance tool `--rejoin on|off`): `Engage.Join` reuses an inactive entry of the same ship (courage, drop share and reload timers stay, no random draw; side, tactic, place, target, refuge new), and `Reinforcements.Helpers` no longer re-adds a helper below its retreat hull (`Reinforcements.RetreatHull`). Effects of the old duplicates (measured, default seed 20000): 56 of 86 pack joins were the same raider re-joining the same fight right after fleeing (two fresh draws and fresh reload timers each time); `Encounter.Of` kept returning the stale first entry, so ship fire and missiles (both look the target up via `Of` and need an active entry) could not hit a re-joined ship — re-joined raiders were effectively immune except to station guns; finish statistics counted the old entries as fled. Side checks and strength sums only use active entries (no double counting). Consequence of the fix: raiders die more often (300000 ticks: 10 → 19 lost) and the pirate treasuries shrank (Blades down to one raider and 0 Cr behind a closed gate 17); calibrated with `fenceShare` 0.75 → 0.85 (default world; loss rate unaffected). Default world 20000 ticks: hash 506C31B3B6B6B902 → D5916A2C8C861567 (twice); before → after (rule off/on, same code): loss rate default 20000 0.33 → 0.50 %, 60000 0.28 → 0.33 %, seeds 1–4 0.52/0.31/0.30/0.25 → 0.40/0.32/0.48/0.37 %, 300000 0.27 → 0.26 %, gate 17 closed (200000) 0.26 → 0.33 %; police 5.0–7.2 % of treasury income, trader bankruptcies 0 (gate run 1), both pirate groups with two raiders at the end of every long run, prices in range everywhere.
- Handy test of round 6: the siege-leave message after the station went down is a victory message (`SiegeWithdrawn.Won`, „Sektor-HQ Beta außer Gefecht — dein Verband (2) folgt dir.“); a real retreat is a toast (the following wanted warning no longer covers it). `WarInfo.RangeStatus(u, st, player, at)` takes the view position (same as „Entfernung“) and says „Geschütze schweigen (ausgeschaltet)“ for a disabled station. A disabled station no longer recharges its shield (hull repair 600/day unchanged — that is the rule). Flight HUD: bays 5–6 start below „Ergeben“ (y 456), hit warning and escape line move below a multi-line toast (`FlightHud.AvoidToast`); selftest stage 85 checks the combat layout with six bays and a long toast.

**Ninth round: tanker and courier in the AI fleet.** `cistern` (class `tanker`) and `swift` (class `courier`) have `jobs: ["trade"]` and a `weapon_laser_s` in a weapon slot appended to their class ("every trader armed"); the classes are 2 lighter, so speeds are unchanged (5.6 / 11.4), power balances valid. `canister_bay_s` holds 60 instead of 30 (a liquid-only bay is the cheap way to move liquids — at 30 the tanker was worse per credit than a universal freighter even for pure liquid demand), so the Cistern carries 360 liquid (3.6 trade capacity units); the shipyard shelf of canister bays is [6, 8] (one tanker needs six; at [1, 3] it was never buyable). Liquids: water, hydrogen, propellant (fuel off: only water and hydrogen). Model choice (`FleetSystem.ChooseModel`) multiplies the trade value by `IShipJob.DemandFactor`: models with only universal holds keep factor 1 (unchanged); solid/liquid-only holds count only up to the unserved demand of their form (`TradeJob.Unserved`: open demand of consumers, shelves and sinks minus what traders already carry or go to buy). Result (default seed and seeds 1–4, 300000, gate 17): two Cisterns bought in the start phase in 6 of 8 runs while liquid demand is unserved, none retired as unprofitable or bankrupt (300000 ticks), water price 0.77–1.0 → 0.6–0.83× base; the courier is never bought (30 solid, its price is mostly the light kit: value per credit 0.105 vs 0.143–0.25 for the other freighters; at the capacity limit there is no room anyway — marginal trade profit of an extra ship is ~0). Migration base and `Phase28Tests.RevertRunde9` restore the v17 state (old fits, no jobs, class slots/mass, canister 30, shelf [1, 3]). Balance tool: line "Abgänge Handelsschiffe je Modell". `OrbitCamera.MaxDistance` 100 → 140 (default view unchanged).

## Phase 29 (English summary): ship roster, every faction buys every model, module tiers, faction tags and name list, contact mining (round 10)

User decisions: no "unit player" (the start ship without a start package is `unit_miner_100`); the skiffs are regular models; every faction, the pirates and the player can buy and use every model (the frigate too); modules can be swapped and upgraded in tiers; faction tags and a large name list; nearly all mining ships use physical tools. Plan, table and progress in `docs/SCHIFFE.md` ("Phase 29: Schiffsraster").

**Roster (30 models, 22 classes).** Unit models stay (founder fleet and most economy tests); `unit_player` is gone (old saves load it as `unit_miner_100`, `WorldBuilder.UnitModelFor(Player)`). Skiff class gets a weapon slot S and is 2 lighter: `pip` trade + mine + laser, `nomad` trade + laser, `prospector` mine. `raider` is no longer restricted. New classes/models: corvette "Hornisse", missile_boat "Lanze", bomber "Donner", gunboat "Keiler", destroyer "Sturmvogel", battlecruiser "Leviathan" (XL, `ShipClass.KitCount` 3 — JSON `kitCount`, kits per ship in `ShipPurchase.Materials`), strip_miner "Maulwurf" (L). German display names for all models (keys unchanged). Patrol craft and frigate get M slots.

**Module tiers.** 11 new modules (shield Mk2/M, armour Mk2/M, laser cannon Mk2/M, mass driver Mk2, launcher Mk2, power bank Mk2, point defence Mk2, reactor M; no platinum in their costs) made by the factories (`weapons_works.maxTier` 2), upgrade paths Mk1 → Mk2 for the combat modules, small yard shelves [0, 1] (larger shelves drew electronics/titanium away from the Mk1 reactor chain: yard Delta reactor empty 70 % of the time).

**Combat model choice (`Simulation/CombatModels`).** Police (`PoliceChoosesModels`), navy (`WarshipChoosesModels`) and pirates (`PirateChoosesModels`) choose among all models without cargo/mining/construction ability and with combat ability: value = strength^(1 + w) ÷ price, w = `Heavy(threat)` (threat ÷ `CombatHeavyThreat` 3; at war 1). Police/navy buy through `ShipPurchase` at yards (`SecuritySystem.CommissionChosen`, `Wars.Commission`); pirates build in the hideout at material value × `PirateBuildFactor` (0.65) × capacity units (S 1, M 1.5, L 2, XL 3) from their treasury, group size in units ≤ `MaxRaiderUnitsPerActor` (default world 2), with a quality roll; `FactionViability.CannotHunt` uses `CheapestPirateCost`. Balance tool: "Kampfschiffe gekauft/gebaut je Modell", "Kampfschiffe jetzt".

**Faction tags and names (F).** `Actor.Tag` (three letters, unique, `ActorTags.Base/Derive`, saved and hashed; Free group "FRE", player "SPL"; `ActorNames.Tag/Tagged` → "[UNI] Name"). `Content/faction-names.json` (embedded, 3391 names: state/guild/corp/outlaw, fixed names plus place×form and adjective×noun) via `Content/FactionNames` with its own seed-derived RNG (`WorldRng`, `ActorRng` — `Universe.Rng` is never touched). World flag `randomNames` (default world true; `TestWorlds.Def` sets false): factions and outlaw actors get random names, station names may use `{owner}` templates ("Hauptquartier – {owner}"); promoted independents take a name from the list. Hash reference constants changed only through the tag mix (verified identical without it): Phase 25 0xFA6521C02E1E130E, Phase 27 0x885FAB4DD2BB8F0F, Phase 28 0x92842D6D5BA8AE3F.

**Labels and accents (G).** Tagged names on 3D labels, edge markers, target/attack cards, dock/station/hangar/sector/faction screens; 3D labels have a fixed screen size (`Gfx.Label` `FixedSize`, pixel size 0.0006, font 32, outline 10) and are hidden when covered by the HUD; station accents saturated with emission 0.35.

**Contact mining (H, user wish "physical mining tools on nearly all mining ships").** `Mining/ContactMining` is the one rule for player (`OreChunks.Contact`) and NPC miners (`MinerSystem`, event `ContactMined`): a tool with module stat `contactMining` (ore per second at a class with chunk chance 0.5, scaled by the class's chunk chance) mines while the bow touches the rock (≤ 35° off the centre, bow ≤ 2.5 from the surface); ore goes straight into the hold in portions of one unit, the ore type per portion rolled by the class weights (one draw), no chunks, no tractor, no recoil, no loss. `ShipStats.ContactMining`, `HasLaser`, `MiningTier` (laser chance × size or contact rate ÷ `ExpectedOrePerSecond`), `UsesContact`; `MineJob.Units/FleetValue` and `MinerSystem.BestMine` use the tier (a failed tool keeps counting with the base values as before). Modules (tool slot, grant Mining, systems works): `drill_head_s` "Bohrkopf S" 3.4 ≈ laser Mk1 (upgrade → `cutting_wheel_s`), `cutting_wheel_s` 5.525 ≈ laser Mk2, `cutting_wheel_m` 8.16 ≈ laser Mk3 (heavy miner tool slot now M), `cutting_drum_l` 10.2 (strip miner). Fits: unit miners, Pickaxe, Prospector → drill; Rockbreaker, Rockbreaker II (with split laser) → wheel S; Quarry → split laser + drill (reactor Mk2); Excavator → wheel M; Maulwurf → drum L. Only the multirole ships (Pip, Tinker) keep lasers. `OreChunks.TryFire` returns `NoLaser` for a ship without a laser. Player: `Main` calls `OreChunks.Contact` every frame for ships with a tool; bay "Bohrer" (`WeaponBays.Contact`, display only: "kein Kontakt · Bug an Fels" / "Abbau läuft" / "Laderaum voll"); sparks at the bow (player) and at the contact point (`ContactMined` of NPCs in the player sector). Auto-mining approaches to `ContactApproachEdge` (10) and pushes on with `ContactPush` (12 % throttle) — far below the ramming threshold `PlayerShip.HardImpact` (14), so contact never damages the hull. `ContactMining.Spin` gives a tool rotation speed for ship models with rotating tools (not wired yet). Save: additive (the NPC progress uses `ShotTimer`).

**Raiders spare the loot.** Rule `RaidersSpareLoot` (default true): in a raid a raider does not fire a weapon whose maximum damage (damage × (1 + spread)) plus the missiles already flying at the target could use up the shield and hull of a victim carrying cargo (pure rule, no draw; not for the active player ship). Cause (measured, 300000 ticks): since pirates buy bombers (two launchers), one missile salvo destroyed skiff miners (hull 60) instead of forcing surrender — miners destroyed 6 → 59. Balance option `--spareloot on|off`.

**Persistence.** Additive (save stays v17): `ActorSave.Tag`, new modules/classes/models as content; the migration base (`SaveGameJson.AfterV17Keys/Fits/Jobs`, skiff/raider/patrol/weapons-works/heavy-miner reversals) keeps the frozen v17 chain. `Phase29Tests.RevertPhase29` (called first by `Phase28Tests.RevertPhase28`) restores the Phase 28 content and rules for the reference-hash tests.

**Tests.** New `Phase29Tests` (raster, buyability, combat model choice, pirate build, module tiers, outfitter quote), `Phase29NamesTests` (9), `Phase29ContactTests` (content and tiers, contact geometry, player contact mining into the hold, bays, NPC contact mining with ore conservation and rate, determinism and save/load) and `Phase29SpareLootTests`. Adjusted on purpose: laser-specific tests refit the ship to `mining_laser_1` (`MiningTests.ToLaser`), the autopilot harness emulates collision and calls `OreChunks.Contact`, module/class/model counts (43/22/30), `MultiroleTests` (rockbreaker wheel, ore counted from `ContactMined`, flexibility rule tested without the prospector), slot/outfitter/fit/capability/damage tests use the tool slot instead of the laser key, `OpenPointsTests` colony luxury median < 0.5 of the samples (measured 378/442/454 of 901), `EconomyFixTests` copper smelter subsidy < 6000 Cr (measured 4425: drills need no copper), `LifecycleTests` ship credits bound, `SecurityTests` police-to-Beta check over the run window, `Phase25Tests` fleet-ship loss with `RaidersSpareLoot` off.

**Selftest.** Stage 14 (auto-mining with the start ship) now mines by contact: checks that contact portions were mined (sparks), the bays are "Bohrer" + tractor without "Laser", and still no hard collision; the laser-bay stages 10–13 fit the Mk1 laser for the check and restore the drill (`Main.TestSetTool`); stage 55 takes a raider that is not in another fight and restores its hull. SELFTEST OK.

**Balance (fresh Release build; before = round 9).** All runs "Preise im Zielbereich" (gate 17 exit 2 only for the split). Default 20000 (hash 4FAE922AA127CF39): loss rate 0.38 → 0.28 %, victims destroyed 2 → 0, police 5.3 → 5.4 % of treasury income, HINWEIS 3 → 4 (laser cannon/drill shelves at the yards, an early ore-starved steelworks, colony luxury); 60000: 0.27 → 0.25 %, HINWEIS 0 → 1; seeds 1–4 at 20000: loss 0.20–0.29 %, victims 0–1, HINWEIS 4–7; 300000: 0.16 → 0.18 %, victims 6 → 7, trade/mine capacity 26.8/26 and 22.3/22, exports food/CG/luxury 312 459/56 302/15 001 (round 9 F/G stage) → 310 414/49 892/13 647; gate 17 (200000): 0.24 → 0.21 %, victims 2 → 0. 0 trader bankruptcies everywhere; both pirate groups keep two raiders. Police buy corvettes, bombers, corsairs and bastions; pirates build corsairs and bombers (no pirate frigate observed). Prospector is the AI's preferred miner (5–11 per run).

**Known weaknesses.** Consumer-goods/luxury exports at 300000 ticks −11 %/−9 % (steel/electronics now also go into drills and combat modules). Trade capacity reached 26.8/26 once (multirole job switch). HINWEIS lines in 20000-tick runs (yard shelves of laser cannons and drills empty in the start phase). The courier is still rarely bought. Rotating tools on the ship models and the label sizes are not verified on a device.

**Round 11 (Phase 29 follow-up): start packages, contact push, name list, early yard shelves, ship models with hardpoints.**
- *Start packages* (user wish "standard ship a fighter, trader or miner"): exactly three regular packages in this order — `fighter` Jäger (Falke, 600 Cr, Delta: yard and sector HQ, next to the pirate dead end Abgrund), `trader` Händler (Nomade, 1500 Cr, Beta hub), `miner` Schürfer (Goldsucher with drill head, 400 Cr, Alpha belts); `generalist` is gone. The start chooser (hamburger "Neues Spiel" and the System area) shows these three and, set apart under "Testszenarien (nur zum Prüfen)", the packages with a `scenario`; the "Standard" card is gone. Without a choice (`BuildDefault()`, tests, headless `new`) the code default stays (unit miner 100, 500 Cr) so hashes do not move.
- *Contact push is not ramming:* the bounce rule of the player ship moved into the Core (`Collision.Bounce` with `Restitution` 0.4, `IsHard` above `HardImpact` 14, `PlayerMaxSpeed` 45; `PlayerShip` uses it). Test `Phase29ContactPushTests`: pushing with `MiningAutopilot.ContactPush` × 45 never exceeds the threshold, ramming at full speed does.
- *Name list:* brand-like corporate names replaced (Polaris-Transporte → Brannoc-Transporte, Helion-Industrien → Thessen-Industrien, Kestrel-Werke → Kestra-Werke), world place names removed from the place pool (Korvan, Borea ~ Borealis); the fixed start faction names stay in the pool on purpose (a name is never given twice). `FactionNames.All` is cached per kind. Only promoted actors' names/tags change, so the reference hashes changed again by the tag mix alone (verified with the old list): Phase 25 0xF1AD6CCD3D90D917, Phase 27 0x02F599DEDC0101B4, Phase 28 0x81DFD311790D8AD1.
- *Early yard shelves (HINWEIS in 20000-tick runs):* the module works lacked inputs 70 % of the start phase, so empty shelves moved from module to module. Changes: yard shelves start full (`reactor_mk1`, `chem_engine_s`, `mining_laser_1` [5, 5], `drill_head_s` [5, 5], `weapon_laser_s` [4, 4]) and the module works keep 20 instead of 10 of each input (`moduleFactory.inputTarget` 20) except platinum (own `stocks` entry [10, 10] at drive, weapons and systems works — at 20 the works drew platinum from the luxury workshop and the colony luxury test failed). Default 20000: HINWEIS 4 → 2, seed 2: 4 → 1 (only the colonies' luxury goods remain; no yard shelf). Migration base and `Phase29Tests.RevertPhase29` restore the v17 values.
- *Ship models* (`World/ShipModels.cs`): `ShipModels.Create(modelKey, accent)` instantiates `res://assets/ships/<key>.glb` once per scene (cache per key), scale = `BaseHalfLength` 2.4 × `ships.json` scale (S 1.0 · M 1.5 · L 2.4 · XL 3.6), bow −Z; material `Accent` = owner colour (`ShipModels.AccentOf`: faction colour, outlaws red, player cyan, others grey), `Engine` = thrust in five shared brightness levels, `Rotor_*` turn with `ContactMining.Spin` (local Z), `Jaw_*` collected (no motion yet); overrides shared per (material, colour/level), shadows off. Used for the player ship (`PlayerShip.SetModel/Animate`, the old primitives stay as fallback) and every NPC marker (`SectorView.SyncShipBody`, rebuilt when the model key changes); distance LOD: beyond `LodDistance` 220 (hysteresis 0.9) from the player the marker shows the old primitive. No model → old primitive.
- *Hardpoints:* `HardpointSet` binds the `hardpoints` of `ships.json` (`slot`) and `stations/stations.json` (`gun`) to a model instance: muzzle and direction converted into the turret node's space (`Turret_<n>`, axis local +Y, barrel −Z), `Muzzle/Direction` in world space, `Aim` turns a turret about its axis towards a target. Missing `hardpoints` → ship/station centre. Core events carry the source additively and without hash/save impact: `ShotFired.Slot`, `ShotAtStation.Slot`, `StationShot.Gun`, `MissileLaunched.Slot`, display-only `MissileFlight.Slot` (not saved). `CombatView` starts beams at `MuzzleOf(ship, slot)`/`StationMuzzleOf(station, gun)` (player shots now get beams too — before they had no start point) and draws missiles rising along the hatch direction (`dir`, often up) for the first 18 % of the flight, then blending into the Core flight path. Turrets of NPCs aim at their encounter target, the player's at the selected target or attacked station, station turrets at their hostile participants (`SectorView.AimStationGuns`). `StationModels` relies only on the `Accent` material name (the new station materials `ShipHull/ShipPanel/ShipArmor` need no change).
- The Android export preset includes `ships.json`, `stations.json` and `gate.json` explicitly (`include_filter`; the models read them with `FileAccess`).
- Selftest stage 91 (`SelfTest.Ships.cs`): player and NPC models (scale, rotors, shared overrides, owner accent), drill turns at the rock, a corsair's beam starts at the muzzle = hardpoint in world space, a bomber's missile starts at its hatch pointing up, a station turret turns towards its target. `--ui-shots <dir> --ui-shots-ships` adds 97 (drilling at the rock) and 98 (raid with models); the gate shots continue with them. Gate labels carry the target sector owner's tag.

**Round 12: more gun positions, music, device-test fixes.**
- *Gun positions (user accepted the recommendation):* Wächter (`warden`, class `patrol_craft`) gets a turret weapon slot S appended (slot 7, `weapon_laser_s`) and `reactor_m` (with `reactor_mk2` the peak load of the third gun exceeded the surplus). Leviathan (`battlecruiser`) 7 → 11 positions: slot 16 `point_defense_s_mk2` (second point defence), appended slots 18/19 weapon M turret `weapon_laser_m`, 20 weapon S turret `weapon_laser_s`, 21 reactor L `reactor_m` (power). Sector HQ guns 2 → 4 (`laser, laser, laser, massdriver`), headquarters 3 → 6 (`laser, laser, missile, laser, laser_m, missile`), shipyard and police station unchanged, pirate hideout gets `defense` (hull 1200, shield 120, armour 50, guns `massdriver, massdriver, laser`). New guns are appended, so existing hardpoint indices stay; until `ships.json`/`stations.json` are regenerated the view falls back to the ship/station centre for the new indices. Rule `PursuitStopsAtHideoutGuns` (true) with `HideoutGunMargin` 15: a pursuit ends ("safe") as soon as target or pursuer is within gun range + margin of the target's hideout — police do not chase into the hideout guns. `StationCombat.Guns` (station attack button, "N Geschütze · R m") counts only faction stations whose gun range reaches the target (device test: Farm Beta showed the sector HQ's guns). War test (three Bastions vs Sektor-HQ Beta) still takes 2–4 min (141 s in the test), alone hopeless. Migration base and `Phase29Tests.RevertRunde12` restore the previous content.
- *Hash constants:* `UniverseHash.Compute(u, includeTags: false)` leaves out the actor tags; the reference tests (Phase 25/27/28) additionally assert that this value equals the pre-Phase-29 constant (0x68F0D589492ADEDC, 0x8BAFCA8A395897AE, 0xCB2A9FDBF34B15AB) — proof that the changed constants depend only on the tags/name list.
- *Music:* `SpaceSim.Core/Audio/MusicState` (pure): `Raw(u, MusicContext(menu, docked, mining))` → Menu > Combat/Siege (siege cause or ≥ 6 active participants) > Warning (pending raid/arrest on the player) > Docked > Mining > Frontier (unowned, threat ≥ 1.5 or security < 0.5) > SafeFlight; `Hysteresis` (rises at once, menu/dock at once, calming down only after 6 s held and 5 s stable); `Key` maps to the `states` table of `music.json`. Tests `MusicStateTests`. Godot `Audio/MusicDirector` (own component): reads `assets/music/music.json` as data, starts all `layer_*`/`mood_*` loops together (silent), blends their linear gains to the state's targets over `crossfade_seconds` (layers and optional layers in dB, the state's mood at 0 dB), stings (`sting_*`) as one-shots with `sting_duck_music_db` ducking; events: bounty → victory, ship lost → loss, jump → jump, war/peace, raid/arrest warning → warning; pauses on app pause. `Audio/AudioSettings`: buses "Musik" and "Effekte" (Sfx plays on "Effekte"), volume in 10 % steps and mute, stored in `user://settings.cfg` (selftest/UI shots use `settings-test.cfg`); UI: System area "Ton" rows (− | Musik 70 % | +, the middle toggles mute) and hamburger menu toggles "Musik"/"Effekte". Import: loops `edit/loop_mode=2` (forward), QOA compression (`compress/mode=2`): imported ≈ 5 MB for all music files (sources ≈ 27 MB are not exported); `music.json` is in the export `include_filter`.
- *Device-test fixes:* in flight the alert sits bottom centre above the throttle (y 448, 536 wide from centre − 280, left of the second weapon-bay column at x 906) and the hit-warning/escape lines right of "RÜCK" (x 570–900, y 534/566) — the forward view where the enemy stands stays free (selftest stage 56 checks the raider's screen position against these rects); squadron formation gap by the largest class (S 7, M 10, L 16, XL 24; `Squadrons.FormationGap`), followers of a squadron led by the player stand in formation around him (`Materializer.Snapshot(…, playerHeading)`), scenario wingmen start in wedge formation with that gap; overlapping 3D labels: the farther one is hidden (selftest stage 91); "Neue Station" toast only in the player's sector; built stations are named type + sector, from the second one with a Roman numeral (`StationFactory.BuiltName`: "Elektronikfabrik Kalypso II"; names are not hashed); switching on auto-mining clears the combat target; camera menu zoom in even steps (`OrbitCamera.ZoomStep`, (140 − 18) ÷ 8 per tap, the maximum 140 is reachable). Player accent on ship models strong cyan with emission 1.4; split jaws (`Jaw_*`) open and close on `AsteroidSplit` (`ShipVisual.SnapJaws`).
- Selftest stage 92 (`SelfTest.Audio.cs`): music loaded and playing, buses exist, safe flight → mining (drilling) → docked → combat give the targets of `music.json` with a soft fade, a sting plays, the System area buttons change bus volume/mute and save to the test settings file, split jaws move, player accent emission ≥ 1.

## Phase 30 (English summary, round 13): station sizes and small stations; colony cash; device-test fixes

Plan and checklist in `docs/STATIONEN.md` (section "Phase 30").

**Sizes (`Model/Economy.cs` `StationSize` XS/S/M/L/XL, `StationSizes`).** `stationTypes[].size` (default M) and optional `radius` (required for XS). Per class: radius S 10 · M 15 · L 22 · XL 30 (XS per type), base hull without `defense` S 600 · M 800 (rule `StationBaseHull`) · L 1400 · XL 2500, max gun emplacements S 2 · M 3 · L 4 · XL 6 · XS 7 (point defence counts for XS), docking S ≤ M ships, M ≤ L, L/XL all, XS none; build cost and upkeep × S 0.9 · M 1 · L 1.1 · XL 1.2. `WorldBuilder` validates (unknown size, XS without radius/defense, XS with services/recipes/stocks, too many guns). Default world: S = office, solar plant, water plant, electrolysis, build site; L = shipyard, trade station, colony, warehouse, sector HQ, company seat, drive/weapons/systems works; XL = headquarters; everything else M. Runtime `Station.Radius`, `Station.Dockable`, `Station.MaxShip` (derived from the type by `StationFactory.Create/ApplySize`, a build site takes the target type's radius and stays dockable; not saved), `DockPoint = Radius + 7`, `SafeRadius(rules)` (0 for non-dockable). The radius replaces the fixed 15 in obstacles, `Universe.StationAt` (edge distance ≤ 15, skips non-dockable), the safety zone (`ThreatMap.NearStation`, `PirateSystem.AtHome`, police home), station guns (reach measured from the hull: `StationCombat.ReachOffset = Radius − 15`), sieges, refuge, belt/split clearance, lifecycle/panic placement (`FindFreeSpot(u, sector, radius)`). `Simulation/Docking` (`SizeOf`, `Allows`, `Issue`) is used by the player dock, traders, miners, best buyer, NPC repair and fuel stations; maintenance and subsidies only for dockable stations. Start world: HQ Alpha (id 32) to (136,−4), Elektrolyse Gamma (13) to (−75,25), Werft Delta (18) to (0,66) (overlaps with the larger radii); `UndockPath.Heading` tries 10° steps.

**Small stations (`Simulation/Outposts.cs`, government kind `outpost` "Kleinstation", size XS, not dockable, not capturable, never a police home base).** Types: `satellite_sensor` (r 2.6, hull 150, security 0.3, 1200 Cr), `platform_light` (r 3.5, 2 lasers, 3000 Cr), `platform_medium` (r 6, laser M + laser + mass driver, point defence, 6500 Cr), `platform_heavy` (r 8, 2 laser M, mass driver, 2 launchers, 14000 Cr); gun order = `tools/blender` emplacements. Start world: satellites in Alpha/Gamma/Aurelia (76–78), light platforms in Alpha/Delta/Hephaistos (79–81). `OutpostDefenseSystem` (Security module, interval 5, rule `OutpostsEngage`): an armed outpost opens a defence fight (`Siege.StartDefense`, no war count, no crime) on a hostile combat ship or the active player in gun reach (war, relation ≤ hostile — outlaws —, player wanted ≥ 3; not while a warning to the player is pending; not raiders at home). `OutpostBuilderSystem` (interval 100, rule `OutpostsBuild`, cooldown 3000 per actor): factions buy outposts as finished modules from `FactionViability.Spendable` (event `OutpostBuilt`, `TreasuryUpkeep` "outpost") — satellites in territory without coverage, light platforms at gates/fields with threat ≥ 1.2, medium at main-route gates with threat ≥ 2.2, heavy only at war; pirates light/medium at their hideout when the treasury keeps 4000; self-built platforms are dismantled after `OutpostCalmTicks` (40000) of calm (reason c "calm"). Persistence additive (save stays v17): `StationTypeSave.Size/Radius`, `StationSave.CalmSince`, `ActorSave.LastOutpostTick`; hashed. The migration base and `Phase29Tests.RevertPhase30` remove sizes, outposts and the moved positions (hash reference tests unchanged).

**Colony cash.** Measured: Kolonie Gamma sat at ≈ 1000 Cr all the time (not only early), 90–97 % of its luxury-empty samples were "poor" while the luxury workshop held 176–187 units — it bought everything at the 2× consumer cap and its income 20 did not cover it. Colony income 20 → 30: luxury empty 2/0/1 of 901 samples on three seeds (before 430/530/409); `OpenPointsTests` bound back to 0.45.

**Device-test fixes (round 13).** Combat camera `Flight/CombatFraming` (pure, tested): frames player and hostile target (fight target ≤ 160 or the announced raider) in the free field, smooth, manual zoom overrides until the fight ends. The combat test scenario was lost by the *second* raider on random seeds ("Neues Spiel" uses a random seed, the Core test used a fixed one; the 6 s death was not reproducible in Core): `ScenarioDef.ReinforceWithoutShield`, reinforcement hull 0.5 — `Startpaket_Gefechtstest_BestehbarMitDauerfeuer` is now a theory over six seeds built like the UI with 4 s reaction time at every raid start, running past the reinforcement. FPS: music step 10 Hz, NPC turret aiming only when engaged, station guns only aimed with a fight in the sector, per-part frame profile (`Main.TestProfile`). Player ship has a cyan ring. Hamburger "Musik"/"Effekte" toggles did nothing (the menu action reached `OnCommandAction`, handled only in `OnMenu`) — fixed. BESCHUSS/escape lines moved away from the RÜCK label. Selftest stage 93 (`SelfTest.Small.cs`): small station in the sector, model radius = type radius for every station, docking refused, the platform fires at a raider from its emplacements (not the centre), combat camera keeps target and ship in the free field at 30 and 60 m, music toggle by tap, prints "Hardpoints geladen" and the frame profile. Stage 69 switches outpost defence off (platforms would bind the patrol).

**Tests.** New `Phase30Tests` (16): sizes/radius/dock point/cost factor, invalid sizes, too many guns, docking rule, placement without overlap, outpost roles and start stations, platform engages a raider (not a trader, switch off), faction builds a light platform under threat and dismantles it after calm, never below the reserve, save roundtrip with outposts, `BuiltName`/`Roman`, `FormationGap`, pursuit ends before hideout guns (theory). Adjusted: the 40000-tick peace test allows fallen pirate outposts; placement/key-point/undock tests use the radius.

**Balance (Release; before = round 12).** All open runs "Preise im Zielbereich", gate-17 run exit 2 only for the split. Default 20000 (twice, hash C33ADBB68F7382FA): loss rate 0.17 → 0.23 %, police 5.3 → 4.9 % of treasury income, 0.40 → 0.44 ms/tick; 11 small stations (5 light platforms, 6 satellites), 7 bought for 15600 Cr (0.9 % of treasury income), 7 defence fights. 60000: loss 0.17 → 0.18 %, 0.243 → 0.271 ms/tick. Seeds 1–3 at 20000: loss 0.12/0.20/0.23 → 0.19/0.13/0.30 %, 13–14 small stations. 300000: loss 0.16 → 0.08 %, police destroyed 11 → 2, miners destroyed 3 → 0, 10 small stations (4 dismantled after calm), outpost upkeep 3.6 % of the official stations' upkeep, 0.182 → 0.197 ms/tick. Gate 17 (200000): 0.14 → 0.11 %. CG export +24 % at 20000, −11 % at 300000 (colony keeps more luxury/CG).

**Correction round 13b (review: pirates died out in long runs).** With round 13 the raids per 20000 ticks fell from 30–65 to 1–25 over 300000 ticks and the Corsairs had 0 raiders from ≈ 100000 ticks: platforms attacked lurking raiders anywhere in gun reach, and pirate platforms at the hideout (cleared by police, rebought without block) took 48 % of the pirate income. Now: `OutpostPirates` false (pirates build no platforms); a platform attacks a raider on its own only within `OutpostCloseRange` (20 from the hull) — in raids/fights in its reach it still fires as a station gun; war enemies in full reach; raiders choose lurk spots outside the reach of hostile platforms (`RaidersAvoidOutposts`, `PirateSystem.PickHunt`). A wanted player (≥ 3, also from the platform's own faction) is first warned like by the police (`PendingEncounter.Station`, saved additively, `PlayerWarningTicks`, surrender to the platform's faction possible; fire only if still in reach after the deadline, else escaped + arrest cooldown). `CombatRefuge` and builder supplier choice only use stations the ship may dock at (`Docking.Allows`). The 40000-tick peace test is strict again (no station falls). Balance after 13b (round 13 → 13b): default 20000 (twice, hash 1324E350C47BF63A) loss 0.23 → 0.34 %, raids 68 → 55, 2+2 raiders; 60000 raids per 20000 68/30/20 → 55/31/37 with 2+2 raiders throughout (round 12: 65/51/32); 300000 loss 0.08 → 0.17 % (round 12 0.16 %), raids per 20000 3–25 → 28–55 (round 12 23–65), Corsairs 0 raiders from 100000 → 2 raiders except one window, "cannot hunt" 0.5 → 0.1 %; seed 2 at 20000 0.13 → 0.28 %; gate 17 (200000) 0.11 → 0.23 %; 12–17 small stations, defence fights 1–5 per run (before 7–25).

**Known weaknesses.** Simulation ≈ 8–15 % slower than round 12 (more stations, size checks). The device's "6 s" death in the combat test was not reproduced; the fix addresses the reproducible loss to the reinforcement. Station guns of normal stations (`StationCombat`) still treat a wanted member of their own faction as friendly (only outposts were changed).

## Phase 31 (English summary, round 14): gate combat (docs/TORE.md, variant "medium"); device-test fixes

User decision: "Torkonzept ohne Neubau von Toren genehmigt" — all six questions of docs/TORE.md as recommended. Checklist at the end of docs/TORE.md.

**Data.** `gateTypes[] { key, name, defense, repairPerDay, maxOutageTicks }` (same defense parser as stations; guns are a data field, empty in the default world): `side` "Nebentor" hull 2000 / shield 300 / regen 3 / armour 70 / point defence 0.2, `main` "Hauptroutentor" 4000 / 500 / 5 / 100 / 0.35. `gates[].type` (default `main` for main routes, else `side`) → `Gate.TypeKey` (saved, not hashed). `Gate.Condition` (`GateCondition`: hull/shield deficits, shield pause, gun timers, `Disabled`, `DownSince`, `LastAttacker`) only on the half with the smaller id — both halves share one pool; saved additively (`GateSave.Type/Condition`, `SaveGame.GateTypes`), hashed only when set. Rule `GateCombat` (code default false; default world true), `GateMaxDown` 1, `GateBlockadesPerWar` 1, `GateReenableShare` 0.25, `GateRepairPerDay` 2000, `GateSplitOutageTicks` 1000 (= `PanicDelayTicks`), `GateMaxOutageTicks` 4800; crime `assault_gate` (heat 4.5, fine 2000). `War.Blockades` (saved/hashed when > 0). `Phase29Tests.RevertPhase31` (called by `RevertPhase30`) removes it all — reference hashes unchanged.

**Seam (`Combat/SiegeTargets.cs`).** `SiegeRef(Kind, Id)` (station or gate) and `ISiegeTarget` (name, sector, position, radius, owner, defender factions, hull/shield/point defence, down, gun range, profile, crime, `Apply`, `Defends`) with adapters `StationTarget` (rules unchanged from `StationCombat`) and `GateTarget` (`Combat/GateCombat.cs`). `Encounter.Gate` (additive next to `Station`; `Encounter.Target` gives the ref), `MissileFlight.Gate`, `TacticDecision.Siege`, `CombatParticipant.TargetSiege` and `AssaultCommand(Sector, Station, Gate = default)`; `Siege.Start(u, ship, ISiegeTarget)`, `Siege.Reinforce` (defenders = owners of both gate ends; AI attacker factions never defend), `CombatSystem` movement/fire/end, `SiegeTactic`, escape block, missiles, `SiegeShot.Shoot` and `PlayerGunnery.TryFireAtGate/StateAtTarget/GateIssue` all go through the seam. Station guns treat attackers of a gate their faction defends as hostile; `GateCombat.Fire` runs gate guns (data only). The Phase 24–30 reference-hash tests stay green.

**Gate out of service.** At hull 0 `GateCombat.Disable`: both halves `GateState.Closed` via `GateAccess.SetState(…, restoring: true)` (routes, map parts and `AccessEnforcement.Revalidate` work unchanged — no ghost orders), war loss booked, event `GateDisabled(splits, outage)`. A manual `SetState(Open)` does not reopen it. `GateRepairSystem` (Combat module, interval 50): shield regen after the pause; outside a siege a defender that is not the last attacker repairs up to the class rate with hull plates at base × markup from `FactionViability.Spendable` only (never below the reserve; `TreasuryUpkeep` "gate_repair", `GateRepaired`); back in service at 25 % (`GateRestored` "repaired") or, without money, after 1000 ticks (map-splitting gates) / 4800 ticks (others) (`GateRestored` "emergency", explicit money source like the emergency commission) — the map never stays split; herrenlose ends simply have no payer. `GateCombat.OutageReason` → `GateAccess.BlockReason`: "Tor … außer Betrieb – läuft in m:ss wieder an" (route planning, autopilot, universe card); `AccessInfo.ForGate` shows it on the gate card.

**AI blockade.** `Wars.PickGateTarget` (NavySystem before station targets): only at war, a typed pair with at least one end owned by an enemy, never map-splitting (`MapParts.SplitsWhenClosed`, which already accounts for gates that are down), only while `GateCombat.DownCount < GateMaxDown`, one blockade per war (`War.Blockades`, counted when the first warship gets the command; a running blockade continues), value = traffic of the enemy end, the half reachable with the fewest jumps. Pirates never attack gates. Note: against the armoured main gates (armour 100) lasers do almost nothing; frigates need their mass drivers/missiles and rearm trips, so an AI blockade is slow (Core test with infinite ammunition: falls; full simulation of the default world: no war, nothing happens).

**Player.** Tapping a gate selects it (autopilot to it as before) and makes it the attack target: target card "Tor … · Hülle · Schild · beschädigt/AUSSER BETRIEB · läuft in m:ss", button "Tor angreifen" (two taps, sub "Verbrechen · Hülle n"), auto-fire + aim assist; shots are booked as `ShotAtGate`, beams end at the gate, hits spark; the first shot starts the siege (`GateAttacked`) and reports `assault_gate` at both end factions; police/warships of the defenders in the sector join. Damaged gates (≤ 50 %) spark; a status sign under the gate name shows "beschädigt (n %)" or "AUSSER BETRIEB · Reparatur n % · spätestens in m:ss"; the closed look is the existing "Closed" model state; alerts/toasts on disable/restore. Siege music state applies (cause Siege).

**Station guns vs wanted members.** `StationCombat.Hostile`: a player wanted (≥ 3) by the station's own faction is a target (as the small stations in round 13b); test.

**Device-test fixes (APK 98a4dbd).** (1) Combat camera: `CombatFraming.Compute(…, FreeRect)` — free field from the actual HUD rectangles of the current resolution (`Main.FramingField`: every HUD rect cuts the side that costs least area; alert included), focus = midpoint of ship and target placed in the centre of the free field (Newton steps), distance until both fit with margin; active up to 240 m (approach); unit test at 2340×1080 for 30/60/100 m; selftest also at 2340×1080 (window resized). (2) Flight alert slimmer (62 px) at y 508 above the slider; the framing keeps its rectangle free. (3) War test: description/scenario for the L-class HQ (approach to ~35 m from the hull ≈ 55 m centre; HQ guns 67 m from centre); target card shows distance to the hull and the centre, and whether the own guns reach (`WarInfo.OwnGunsText`, appended to `RangeStatus`); toast "Dein Verband greift mit an"; Core test with the realistic stand-off 56 m (2–4 min). (4) Siege odds count only the target itself, stations whose guns cover it and police/warships within 150 m; small stations have their own short line (kind, hull/shield, guns, "nicht andockbar"). (5) Target card distance to the hull for stations; near L/XL stations the default camera zooms out by 1 + (radius − 15)/40. (6) Offline catch-up: 80 ms of ticks per frame on the loading screen (was 30) — the slowdown since round 12 is spread over all systems (more stations), the docking check was the only hotspot (fixed in round 13).

**Tests.** New `Phase31Tests` (15): values/assignment/validation, shared pool, disable closes both halves and re-plans trips, emergency restart 1000/4800, repair from spendable money only and re-enable at 25 %, route reason, AI blockade rules (theory over all pairs incl. a pair already down, one per war), AI frigates take a gate down without splitting the map, player attack = crime at both ends + police defend, save/load mid-siege bit-identical, switch off / no gate types = same hash, 40000-tick run with repeatedly disabled gates (gates come back, factions keep police/seat, trade capacity ≥ 80 %), wanted member hostile to station guns. `OrbitCameraTests` +3 (asymmetric free field). Adjusted: `Phase28Tests` (war-test stand-off 56 m, range line starts with the old text, warships may get a gate blockade first), `Phase23Tests` long run checks `Recovers` (ships that can recover) instead of `NeedsRecovery`. Selftest stage 94 (`SelfTest.GateCombat.cs`): war test, select gate, "Tor angreifen", shots hit the gate, crime at both ends, target card, gate out of service (closed model, status sign, gate card, route reason), restart after the deadline; stage 93 extended (2340×1080 framing).

**Balance (Release; before = round 13b).** Default world with `GateCombat` on: bit-identical to 13b and to `--gatecombat off` (no war → nothing attacks gates): 20000 twice hash 1324E350C47BF63A (0.49 ms/tick), 60000 32705D79596B78A5, 300000 2FCD3417375C4360 (0.209 ms/tick); all "Preise im Zielbereich", 0 HINWEIS. Test driver `--gatedown 20000` (every 20000 ticks the next non-splitting pair goes out of service, 300000 ticks): 14 outages, at most 1 at a time, all 14 repaired by their owners in 993 ticks on average (max 1150), repairs 144 000 Cr, 0 trader bankruptcies during an outage, loss rate 0.10 %, prices in range. Balance tool: line "Torkampf", options `--gatecombat on|off`, `--gatedown N`, `--gatedownsplit on`.

**Correction round 14b (review: a dead-end gate could be kept down permanently).** The emergency restart set the gate to 25 % hull even under fire, so continuous fire knocked it out again within seconds and `DownSince` restarted. Now every restart (repair or emergency) starts a grace period (`GateCondition.GraceUntil`, rules `GateRestoreGraceTicks` 2400 / `GateSplitRestoreGraceTicks` 6000 for map-splitting gates): the gate is invulnerable and not a target (`GateCombat.InGrace`, `GateTarget.IsDown`, `PlayerGunnery.GateIssue` "Notbetrieb – nicht angreifbar für m:ss", AI blockade skips it), the shield is full, running sieges of the pair end cleanly at the restart. The emergency deadline still runs during a siege. `Restore` keeps the state from before the outage (`GateCondition.PrevState`: a gate closed by hand stays closed). Saved additively, hashed only when set. UI: "Tor angreifen" stays visible but disabled with the reason; the target card shows "Notbetrieb, unangreifbar m:ss". Tests: continuous fire over ≥ 3 cycles on gate 17 (every open phase ≥ the grace period, down < 30 % of the time), grace/shield/siege end/save, manually closed gate stays closed; selftest stage 94 checks the disabled button. Default world hash at 20000 ticks unchanged.

**Known weaknesses.** AI blockades are slow against armoured gates (lasers barely hurt armour 100; frigates go home to rearm, the owner repairs meanwhile) and only happen in worlds with war. The device's 6-second death (round 13) and the framing at 2340×1080 are verified in the headless selftest by resizing the window, not on the device.


## Round 15 (English summary): device-test fixes for gate combat and the combat camera

- **Combat camera.** `CombatFraming.ComputeCombat` (pure, tested): the smallest distance from `OrbitCamera.MinDistance` at which ship and target (target with its body radius — station/gate attacks) lie in the free field and their distance fills at most `FillShare` (0.75) of it; the box of both (with margins) is centred in the free field. Cap 90 × ship-size zoom for ships (above: only the ship, centred in the field, the target keeps its target arrow), `MaxDistance` × zoom for station/gate attacks. Active from the raid warning on (the announced raider becomes the target at the warning; framing up to 400 m). Focus returns quickly after the fight and the camera never lets the own ship leave the free field (`Main.ShipInFreeField` pulls the focus back). Default camera distance scales with ship size (S 1, M 1.25, L 1.6, XL 2.1).
- **Gate values recalibrated** (cause: per-hit armour absorption ate laser hits; missiles empty after ~30 s): side armour 70 → 30, main hull 4000 → 3500 and armour 100 → 25 (docs/TORE.md "Runde 15"). Core test with the real war-test squadron and limited ammo: side gate 2.3 min, main gate 6.7 min, a lone Falke not within 6 min. Only gates use these values — the default-world hash is unchanged.
- **Gate tap only selects** (`Main.SelectGate`; autopilot/jump/attack are separate buttons); the sector-view gate card shows hull/shield/state (`GateInfo.Short`).
- **Target card**: the situation and combat lines wrap to two lines (font 14) instead of being cut; small stations show "Hülle · Schild · n Geschütze/Sensor · nicht andockbar" (kind in the name). The odds for a Falke against a light platform are correctly "hopeless" (platform 2 lasers + 450 hull + 80 shield vs Falke 70 hull + 40 shield).
- **Approach automation** (`Flight/AttackApproach`, pure, tested): during a station/gate attack with "Ziel folgen" the ship flies to 0.7 × the range of its shortest non-missile weapon from the hull (min 12 m) and holds (reverse if too close); slider/turn input overrides. War test: victory with ≥ 30 % hull asserted.
- **"Dauerfeuer" pulses** (warn frame, brightness) while a raid is announced or running and auto-fire is off.
- **Selftest stage 95** (`SelfTest.Phone.cs`, window 2340×1080): warning phase (target set, framing active, ship in field, auto-fire pulse), combat (both framed, distance fills ≥ 30 % of the field, target card complete), after the fight (ship stays in the field), gate tap selects without autopilot, station attack (HQ on screen, ship in field, approach throttle, target card complete).

## Round 16 (English summary): combat camera on the real device path, target card, gate selection, ammo honesty, running costs, memory

- **Camera cause (reproduced in a non-headless run at the device aspect 1560×720, `--phone-shots <dir> --camdebug`, tool `godot/scripts/PhoneShots.cs`; images `/home/gerd/space-adventure-scratch-archiv/r16/vorher` and `nachher`):** (a) the free field was cut greedily on the "cheapest side" — the right button column ("Nächster Gegner"/"Ziel folgen") cut off the top, leaving 35 % of the height, so at 31 m only the ship was framed; (b) during the warning the far raider had no view marker, so there was no frame and no arrow; (c) "Nächster Gegner" cycled from the attacker at 30 m to the reinforcement raider at 380 m. Fixes: pure `Flight/FreeField.Largest` (largest empty rectangle around the screen centre, tested) for `Main.FramingField` (from the real HUD rects + alert, cached 0.2 s); target positions fall back to `PlayerCombat.PositionOf`; `PlayerCombat.NextEnemy` cycles only among the fight's enemies while fighting (keeps the only one); the warning sets the raider as target; red target arrow at the free-field edge when the target is outside it. Diagnose line ("Kamera-Diagnose" in the hamburger menu or `--camdebug`): framing on/off, reason, field, distance, target.
- **Target card**: no autowrap, compact texts (`WarInfo.RangeShort` "Geschütze 67 m: draußen · Rak. ✓", `OwnGunsShort`, `GateInfo.Short` "AUSSER BETRIEB · m:ss"/"Notbetrieb · unangreifbar m:ss"), overflow measured with the font (`FlightHud.TestTargetLineOverflow`). 3D label hiding counts multi-line labels with all lines (`Main.LabelBox`).
- **Gate selection**: own toast for gates; "Neues Spiel" clears the selected gate (no stale "Tor angreifen"); the command-centre sector view "Ziel wählen" on a gate gives the same target card (`SectorScreen.GateTargetRequested` → `Main.SelectGate`).
- **Gate timing honesty** (docs/TORE.md "Runde 16"): main gate 6.3 min at 40 m, 7.4 min at 55 m (mass driver silent), never from 70 m+ after the missiles (40 s); ammo empty after 4.5 min. Values unchanged; the target card shows `PlayerGunnery.DepletedText` ("Raketen leer · Munition leer – nur noch Laser") plus a one-time toast with the rearm place; the war-test description names times and distances.
- **Edge arrows** sit inside the free field; stations under HUD elements also get an arrow (tap selects).
- **Running costs**: `Hangar.UpkeepShips/RunningCostsPerMinute/RunningCostsText` — the war test's two wingmen pay fleet upkeep (2 × 0.01 Cr/tick = 4.8 Cr/min), shown in the HUD credits line ("· −4,8/min") and in "Meine Fraktion" ("Laufende Kosten"; the placeholder is gone).
- **Selftest**: stage 95 taps "Nächster Gegner"/"Dauerfeuer" through the viewport input and requires the attacker to stay the target, target in the free field or arrowed; no "Tor angreifen" after a new game; sector-view gate target = tapped target; gate out of service card complete. Stage 87 adds a combat-test fight and a gate outage/restart per round: nodes 1825 → 1825, objects 6327 → 6327, static memory +1 MB between rounds 1 and 2 (no view leak).
- **Tests**: new `Runde16Tests` (free field, next enemy stays the attacker, depleted text, running costs equal the measured wallet drain).

## Phase 32: mining anchors (round 17, English summary) — harpoons and tethers for contact mining; combat-camera rest points; session memory

- **Rule (`Mining/MiningAnchor.cs`, pure, no random draw; switch `EconomyRules.MiningAnchors`, code default false, default world true; `Phase32Tests.RevertPhase32`).** A contact miner in bow contact fires its harpoons (`Attach`, event `AnchorFired`); after `AnchorTicks` (7 = 1.75 s) the anchor holds (`AnchorHeld`) and only then does `ContactMining.Step` mine (player `OreChunks.Contact` returns the new state `ContactState.Anchoring` meanwhile; NPC `MinerSystem` the same per tick). An anchored ship lies at the (shrinking) rock surface (`HoldPosition`, the view pins the player ship: `PlayerShip.Pin`). Breaking (`Release`, event `AnchorReleased` with reason): own throttle above `AnchorBreakThrottle` (0.3) or any turn input (`BreaksFromSteering`; the auto-mining push 0.12 holds; too much throttle at contact does not fire at all), the tool bay ("Lösen"), a hard impact, any hull hit (`Hit.Apply` → `OnHullHit`), combat start (`Engage.Join` → `OnCombat`: the participant cuts the tethers and does not move for `AnchorCastOffTicks` (6) — `CombatParticipant.CastOffUntil`, saved/hashed when set), rock depleted or split (`AsteroidSplitter` → `OnSplit`), contact lost, hold full, departure (`Travel.Start`). State `Ship.AnchorRock`/`AnchorProgress` saved additively and hashed only when set. `AnchorRateFactor` (1) would compensate the anchoring time on the tool rate; measured unnecessary.
- **Ore flow (balance tool line "Erzabbau", option `--anchors on|off`):** NPC contact ore 20000 ticks 98 325 → 99 185 (+0.9 %), 60000 263 205 → 272 430 (+3.5 %), 300000 1 248 612 → 1 266 313 (+1.4 %); delivered 79 597 → 80 043 / 197 182 → 200 117 / 900 148 → 922 442. Mining is demand-bound, the 1.75 s per rock (≈ 4 % of miner time) does not reduce the flow, so tool rates, `ExpectedOrePerSecond` and capacity units stay. With the switch off the hashes equal round 16 (20000 1324E350C47BF63A, 60000 32705D79596B78A5, 300000 2FCD3417375C4360); with anchors: 20000 F27F5C7D53338CBD (twice), 60000 1DCFF5A775EE6BFC, 300000 F17962C635C2D3E4. All runs "Preise im Zielbereich", no trader/miner bankruptcies.
- **Auto-mining** (`MiningAutopilot`): approach → "Ansetzen" → "Anker fliegt" → "Abbau (verankert)" → releases before selling or changing rock (adopts the rock its harpoons hang on as target).
- **View (`World/AnchorView.cs`, pooled):** harpoons fly from the `Harpoon_*` nodes (`ShipVisual.Harpoons`, model tips hidden) to the impact point on the rock surface following the Core progress; tethers are thin cylinders; release/break snaps them back (break: sparks); while mining a debris plume at the contact point and thruster puffs at the sides, slight engine glow; NPC miners in the player sector face their rock and show the same; fallback without harpoon nodes: two tethers from the bow. Tool bay shows "Anker fliegt …" (progress bar) / "verankert · Abbau" / break reason ("Riss: Treffer") and is named "Lösen" while anchored.
- **Combat camera rest points:** `CombatFraming.ComputeCombat` keeps ship and target at least `EdgeShare` (12 %) of the free field from its edge; in the warning phase the camera frames the announced raider up to 200 × size (fill share 1, edge 8 %) — the 120 m approach is visible; "Nächster Gegner" and the framing only consider announced raiders (`PlayerCombat.Announced`, within the warning time), not a later reinforcement; station edge arrows avoid the target and its arrow, at most three during warning/fight (`Hud.EdgeThin`); edge labels and the target arrow label avoid toast/alert; the 2D target ring and 3D ship rings are hidden under HUD buttons; in warning/fight the toast moves to the left column below the power buttons; in flight it ends before the right button column. `FreeField.Largest` edge cases tested (area on the centre line, full-width HUD, no free band).
- **Device-test follow-ups:** short texts ("Rak. leer · Mun. leer · nur Laser", "Verbrechen", "Notbetrieb 7:59", "Sprung nach …"); the command-centre sector view opens on the player sector unless the universe map chose one, a different sector clears the station/gate choice (autopilot followed a stale gate); gate restart is an alert in the player sector; gate labels draw in front of the model; running costs show upkeep and berth fee separately; ships following in formation pay no berth fee (`Hangar.InFormation` — the −16.8 instead of −4.8 Cr/min: wingmen ending at a foreign dock point).
- **Memory:** the dock menu's ship chips held the previous universe after a lost ship (`DockMenu.ResetSession`); contexts, star map and dock context are reset in `AttachSession`; `Fx.Burst` shares mesh and material per colour. Selftest stage 97: five "Neues Spiel" with different packages (with fights) → of the sessions created in the stage only the current universe lives (`Main.AttachedUniverses`, weak references); also fixed: built command-centre/dock screens kept the old universe in their button closures (`DropContent` on session change).
- **Tests:** new `Phase32Tests` (anchoring before mining, every break reason, split, cast-off in combat incl. save/hash, save mid-anchoring bit-identical, auto-mining harness, NPC ore flow and switch-off hash), `Runde16Tests` + free-field edge cases. Selftest stage 96 (`SelfTest.Anchors.cs`): anchoring with tethers at the harpoon nodes, tips in the rock surface, plume, pinned ship, break by thrust and by a hit, auto-mining anchors without a hard impact; stage 97 session memory. `--phone-shots <dir> --phone-mining` takes mining images (m01–m04).

## Round 18 (English summary): device-test fixes for mining anchors and the warning-phase camera

- **Bay texts fit the 176-px button:** contact bay "verankert" / "Anker fliegt" / "Bug an Fels" (also without anchors), short break reasons (`MiningAnchor.ShortReason`, e.g. "Riss: Wenden"), missile bay place "12/12 Rak. · 110 m". `KitButton.Overflow()` measures every line with the font like the drawing; `FlightHud.TestBayOverflow`/`TestBayLineFits` — selftest stage 96 checks all contact states × anchors on/off, every break reason and missile/weapon places.
- **Break reason:** turning has its own reason `turn` (`MiningAnchor.SteeringBreak`: turn before thrust); the reason shows on the bay for 2.5 s of game time (`Main._anchorBrokeLeft`, was wall-clock) and is cleared by the next `AnchorFired`. Selftest presses the real "◀" button.
- **Auto-mining off keeps the anchor** (checked by tapping the real "Auto-Mining" button in stage 96: anchor stays, ore keeps coming); only release button, thrust, turning etc. break it.
- **Warning-phase camera:** zoom capped so the own ship keeps `CombatFraming.MinShipScreenShare` (6 %) of the screen height (`CombatFraming.MaxDistanceForSize`, `Main.WarningCap`: S ships ≈ 86 m); a raider that does not fit gets the target arrow; the normal edge share applies. Stage 95 checks the distance cap and that neither ship nor raider sits under the alert.
- **Drill visibility:** rotors get an emissive off-axis marker (`ShipModels.AddRotorMarker`), rotor speed 14 → 8 rad/s, spin-up 0.6 while anchoring (`ContactMining.Spin`), camera 0.7× closer while anchored (`Main.AnchorZoom`).
- **Berth-fee loophole closed (player ships only):** `Hangar.InFormation(u, ship)` — followers are free of the berth fee only while their leader is not docked itself; a squadron parked with its leader at a foreign station pays (`Runde18Tests`). NPC hash unchanged (F27F5C7D53338CBD).
- *Known weakness (not changed):* a leader hovering next to a foreign station without docking leaves its followers at the dock point free of the berth fee; chains of leaders (a follower following a follower) are not resolved. **Closed in Round 36** (one berth-fee rule: any player ship docked at a foreign station pays; followers in formation have no dock).

- **Start package `anchor` "Ankertest (Test)"** (data-driven, new `ScenarioDef` fields `atRock` (asteroid class: start with the bow at the largest minable rock of that class in the start sector, first free one of 16 fixed directions), `rockOre` (ore of that rock), `calm` (no raids on the active player ship in this save: switches the saved rule `PlayerRaids` off; fleet raids stay)): Goldsucher with three extra cargo slots (hold 500) in Alpha at a metal rock with 2000 ore; the view turns the bow to a rock the contact miner starts at (`Main.AttachSession`), so it anchors without input and mines ≈ 2.4 min before the hold is full. ## Round 20 (English summary): device-test fixes of Round 19 — target switches, followers not docked, anchor adoption and manual release

- **Target switches and context button:** a ship chosen as target (`Main.SelectShip`, also used by the tap path) ends gate/station selection and attack ("Tor angreifen" stayed after Tor → Schiff); an autopilot keeps its navigation target. The context button follows the pure rule `Flight/ContextChoice.Pick(nearStation, nearGate, selectedStation, selectedGate)`: a selected gate in range wins over a nearby station ("Andocken: <Station>" stayed with a gate selected), a selected station over a nearby gate, otherwise station before gate as before.

The bay names the real reason through `PlayerCombat.TargetIssueShort` ("eigenes Schiff", "Ziel angedockt", "kämpft mit dir", "anderes Gefecht").

Player path only (NPC miners use `MinerSystem`).

- Empty target card text "Station, Tor, Fels antippen" (fits; was cut). Selftest failure end stage is now 999 (stage 99 exists).
- Tests: `Runde20Tests` (context rule, followers not docked + reason, anchor adoption, manual release, `atRock` error paths: unknown class, no rock of the class in the start sector); selftest stage 99 (`SelfTest.Round20.cs`: switches Tor → Schiff → Tor → Station → Tor with target card and attack buttons, context button at a gate next to a station, empty card fits, auto-mining keeps the anchor, "Lösen" → "gelöst"/"Ankern" without re-anchoring, "Ankern" anchors again). War-test squadron with two patrols near: "Knapp" (≈ 120 : 90); the Bollwerk alone stays "Aussichtslos allein".
