## Round 36 (English summary): two-goods leak, command capacity through squadrons, berth-fee loophole

Three open points (user decision "Handel so lassen" still binding: player-only rules, NPC economy unchanged — default hash D3567DA325B7FED9).

- **Two-goods leak (closes the Round 32 "Remaining leak").** Cause: buying a station's product P raises P's own scarcity price, and through the willingness ceiling (`MarketSystem`, product value = own price when nobody else bids, else min(own, bid)) the station's bid for P's inputs jumps (Werft Hephaistos: 4 medium kits → hull plates 108.5 → 400 at unchanged stock). Rule (`EconomyRules.BuybackInputCap`, default true; `PlayerLedger.InputCap`): while the player actor has an open purchase post for a product P of station S, every sale of an input Z of a recipe of S that makes P (any quantity, any own ship, fleet bookings via `BookSale`) gets at most the bid S would have **without his purchase** — the ceiling recomputed by the `MarketSystem` rule (`CounterfactualCeiling`, `BestBid` per good) with P's own price taken at stock + the purchased units the station has not produced back yet. New post fields `LedgerPost.MissingQty`/`MissingSeen` (a purchase adds, every stock increase of the station refills them first in `PlayerLedger.Prune`; saved additively in `LedgerSave`, hashed only when > 0); when the ceiling binds the cap is market × (counterfactual ÷ recomputed ceiling), so a small difference of the recomputation (previous-tick bids) never caps by itself; no cap when the purchase does not change the ceiling. Ship purchases book the consumed kit as a purchase (`OnBought`) and are covered; selling Z at another station stays free (arbitrage). Notes: "Wegen deines Kaufs von <P> hier: höchstens p je Einheit, bis Preis und Lager zurück sind" / short "Deckel p" (existing `SaleNote/SaleNoteShort`, no screen change). **First built as the requested snapshot** (bid before the first purchase, stored per post): closed the leak equally but cost the honest player fleet 36 % (9 ships, 40 000 ticks, seed 11: wallet 401 921 → 257 788, 507 capped sales, ≈ 184 k Cr; fleet posts stay open for a long time and the old snapshot also capped natural input price rises); a first counterfactual with "missing = since the first purchase" still cost 15 % (NPC purchases counted as the player's); the refill rule costs nothing measurable. **Proof** (`space-adventure-scratch-archiv/r36`, program `p` modes `two36` (every product→good pair of every station; variants: one booking after 1 tick, same tick, two bookings in the same tick, 5 ticks, second ship sells, all products bought, purchase over 5 ticks, 3 sales over 3 ticks, purchase with ship B and sale 20 ticks later), `ship36` (every model at every yard, then every yard good), `detect36` (buy anything anywhere, then sell at every market entry whose price rose), metric = best honest timing against an undisturbed twin world): product→input, without → with the rule, max per attack: seed 11 14 283 → 173 (1 M station cash) / 1 749 → 24 (real cash), seed 3 13 992 → 60 / 1 470 → 24 (continuous variant v7: 13 924 → 28 with 1 M cash, 2 901 → 0 with real cash), seed 5 14 283 → 311 / 1 586 → 60; cases > 0: 91–185 → 5–34 per variant; ship purchase → input 1 329 / 163 → 0 everywhere; "all products" → other inputs 9 116 → 93. Remaining cases (dissected with mode `dbg36`): (a) the purchase un-pauses the product's recipe, the station consumes the input and its stock price rises (e.g. Kraftwerk Alpha hydrogen 57 → 56 units, +24 Cr; Systemwerk Beta electronics 19.7 → 17.6, +311 with 1 M cash) — the same effect any NPC purchase of P has, the ceiling does not bind there; (b) NPC reaction paths (the same titanium rise at the weapons works Gamma after buying armour at three different yards); (c) ≤ 2 % recomputation offset in the first tick. Honest trade: fleet automation 9 ships 40 000 ticks seeds 11/3 wallet 401 921 / 401 834 (without) → 408 584 / 429 373 (path noise; 90 / 97 capped sales, 34.6 k / 27.4 k Cr below the market price the fleet itself had raised); reviewer mode `honest` identical with and without; `honest36` (buy 10 P, wait, sell 30 carried Z): extra loss +41 Cr (100 ticks), 0 (400), +62 (1500) of 5 415 natural; the self-made gain right after the purchase 6 528 → 183 Cr. Fleet test (`FlottenTests.Flotten_Automatik…`, 2/4 traders + 2 miners, 40 000 ticks): 40/81 capped input sales, 7.0 k / 10.8 k Cr below the market price the fleet itself had raised (≈ 2–3 % of the profit); the wallet moves by path (2 traders 362 770 → 310 507, 4 traders 370 147 → 400 941) and on the 4-trader path one NPC trader goes bankrupt (0 without the cap; NPCs have no posts) — the assertion allows at most one. **This is a real, small cost for honest trade** (a fleet that buys a product at a station and delivers its inputs there no longer earns from its own price impact); `BuybackInputCap` false switches it off.
- **Command capacity through squadrons.** Reproduced: squadron, work command to all members, capacity filled, disband → members kept single commands above the capacity (repeatable). Rule (`Squadrons.LeaveIssue/DisbandIssue`, player squadrons only): leaving or disbanding is refused when the ships keeping a command (anything but "follow the leader") would push single commands above the capacity (or above the current number when the command level has dropped — Round 25: running commands stay); message "Auflösen gesperrt: danach n Einzelbefehle, erlaubt m – erst bei k Schiffen im Verband den Befehl beenden." `Squadrons.Leave/Disband` now return bool (`out string` overloads; NPC callers unaffected); fleet screen shows the refusal. Leader loss (`OnShipRemoved`) never adds single commands: the successor stays in the squadron. Over capacity now only happens when the command level drops.
- **Berth-fee loophole.** One rule `Hangar.PaysBerthFee` (display and booking in `HangarSystem`): a non-active player ship (no rental, not travelling) with `DockedAt` at a foreign station pays — `InFormation` no longer exempts (kept for display). Followers in formation have no dock: `FollowExecutor` checks on arrival at once (player ships), and a player follower docks only when its chain of leaders really docks there (`DocksAt`, chains up to 8; a follower of a follower used to dock at the dock point its leader only flew by). NPC escorts unchanged.
- Tests `Runde36Tests` (14): measured Hephaistos case with real tick (cap on/off), second ship/fleet booking/two bookings/tick border, other station and unrelated good free, several products, no cap without own influence, ship purchase, expiry, save/load + notes; capacity refusal and release, repeated bypass never above the limit, leader loss; hovering leader with chain (no dock, no fee) vs docked = pays, flying vs docked squadron. Adjusted: `Runde18Tests` (a docked follower of a flying leader pays now), `Runde25Tests` (over capacity through a dropped command level instead of disbanding), `Runde30Tests` honest-fleet invariance runs with the input cap off (its cost is measured separately), `FlottenTests.Flotten_Automatik…` allows one NPC bankruptcy (see above). Fixed during the round: the refill step in `Prune` first skipped the expiry check of that post in the same tick (no measurable effect; corrected).

## Round 37 (UI stage E5, English summary): device-test r35 fixes, menu layer, ui-check with safe-area inset

- **Buttons:** `KitButton` shrinks the main line to the layer's font floor before using "…" (fits "Station angreifen" at large); left-aligned buttons with sub/detail lines keep 4 px top/bottom.
- **Trade editor:** `TradeScreen.Editor.ButtonRow(w)` lays out −/qty/+/Max/Kaufen/Verkaufen from the row width (old row from 662 px; below, "Menge" drops and the fields narrow) — "Verkaufen" ran past the row on the A15 at large. The tool missed it because no state opened a row and checks ran without safe-area insets: new state `dock-handel-zeile`, new size "Samsung A15 mit Kamera-Aussparung links" (`--ui-check-sizes=2340x1080a`, left inset 54 logical px, state prefix `aussparung-`), plus a rule "button sticks out sideways of its nearest laid-out ancestor". Counter-check with the old layout reports the clip.
- **Taps vs swipes:** `UiKit.OnTap` fires on release only when press and release lie on the row within the 12-px dead zone and no swipe runs or ended this frame (`KitScroll.Swiping`); used by factions, comms and assets rows.
- **Command centre:** factions narrow = list (full height) or detail with "← Liste"; detail cards with fixed button footer and scrolling text; rail scroll position shared by all areas (`CommandScreen.RailPos`), active entry kept visible; `KitScroll` draws a "more below" gradient/arrow; shipyard cards grow with the material list, NBSP before "%"; sector map fits the content bounding box (horizontal stretch up to 1.4, rim clipped to the map), labels fall back to smaller font, leader line (`LabelLayout.DrawWithLeader`), first word; hangar rental offer wraps.
- **E5:** hamburger menu and star map in their own full-area layer (`Hud._menuLayer`, `UiScale.MenuFactor` = stage factor); menu window centred, at most the area's height; system rows built from visible buttons only. Decision: no global content scale factor (per-layer factors stay; world overlays and picking keep screen pixels).
- **Guard/perf:** `Runde35cTests` scans all of `godot/scripts` for every text-draw call (incl. multiline/outline/char, space before "("); perf line measures the production `FitName` cache (cold 0.54 ms, warm 0.004 ms, uncached 1.92 ms for 54 rows).
- Selftest stage 112 (`SelfTest.Round37.cs`). Simulation unchanged (hash D3567DA325B7FED9).

## Round 37b (English summary): back paths, Android back key, rail scrolling, frame width, ui-check exit code

- **Blocker fixed:** closing the star map opened from the docked menu left the bare 3D scene (nobody handled `MapOverlay.Closed`). `Main.ShowBase()` is the single way back (docked → dock menu, else flight HUD) for map close/sector pick, menu close/backdrop, "← Flug", emergency, economy. Android back no longer quits (`application/config/quit_on_go_back=false`); `Main.HandleBack()` (WM_GO_BACK_REQUEST, Escape on desktop) closes map/menu/command centre, returns from a dock area to the dock menu, hints in the dock menu, opens the menu in flight.
- **Rail:** active entry is brought into view only when the area or rail height changes; `RailPos` follows the real (pending-aware) position; the rail owns its position (`KitScroll.OwnPosition`, skipped by Capture/Restore); Restore also restores 0.
- **Frame width:** whole-area scrolls in command centre and dock hide their scrollbar (`ShowNever`), which widened the centre frame by ~18 px; `CenterCheck` compares the frame with the shell size (ui-check reports it as "outside").
- Sector map per-axis scale (x stretch up to 2.5), leader lines avoid crossings; narrow faction detail buttons on the top row; star map has a bottom message strip for alert/toast.
- **ui-check:** real A15 insets for `2340x1080a` (80/84/135 device px → 53/56/90 logical); drawn texts count as content under alert/toast; new states `flug-stationswahl`, `kz-fraktionsdetail`, `sternenkarte-toast`; exit code 1 and "UI-CHECK FEHLER" on findings (`UiCheck.AllowedTruncations` lists justified exceptions). The `[ui] Safe Area` log line also prints the raw `GetDisplaySafeArea`.
- Selftest stage 113 (`SelfTest.Round37b.cs`): every back path via real taps/keys, rail swipes both ways, frame width per area; stage 112 swipes the scrollable factions list. Mutations (OnTap on press, no map-close handler, rail re-centred every rebuild, visible scrollbar) each turn the stage red.

## Round 37c (English summary): back key handled once per press, inner layers first, safe area everywhere, lost dock station

- **Back key blocker:** one press of Android back was delivered twice (2–16 ms apart). `Main.BackRequested(source)` handles a press once; further deliveries within 6 frames (notification or `Key.Back`) are dropped and logged. Escape only on desktop. Back closes the innermost layer first: alert, star map, menu, narrow faction detail (→ list), start-package chooser, command centre, open trade row, dock area (→ dock menu); never quits.
- **Lost dock station (review M1):** if the docked station disappears, the view leaves the docked state, shows the flight HUD and an alert (view only, hash unchanged). `ForgetRailArea` is called when the command centre closes (M2).
- **Safe area (M3):** star map, hamburger window, flight alert/toast search, edge-marker names and every alert/toast stay inside the safe area. ui-check reports any button or text (also drawn, alert, toast) outside it; sizes `2340x1080a` (runtime inset from the device log: left 80 px → 53 logical) and `2340x1080d` (dumpsys: also top 84, right 135) are distinguishable in logs/files (`Finding.Key`); the truncation exception is bound to `dockmenue-trade_station`.
- Sector map: whole symbol areas reserved for leader lines, more spacing, selection changes colour only (labels stay put — selftest compares positions).
- Selftest 113 simulates each back press via notification + key event + a second notification next frame and requires exactly one handling (mutation without debounce: 19 failures); also back with alert, start chooser, narrow faction detail, open trade row, taxi, and a docked station removed.

## Round 37d (English summary): back debounce with a time limit, HUD re-laid on inset change, back ignored during dock animation

- A second back delivery is dropped only within 6 frames **and** 150 ms (`Main.BackDebounceMs`, injectable `Main.BackClock`), so quick real double presses at low frame rates still count.
- `FlightHud.ApplyScale` also re-lays the HUD when the safe-area insets change at the same factor/size (rotation, multi-window); this was the cause of the non-reproducible ui-check subset finding.
- Back during the dock/undock animation (`_transition`) does nothing. Selftest 113 covers low frame rate, 10-ms double delivery and back during the dock animation; mutations of both rules turn it red.

## Round 38a (English summary): fixes from the final device test

- Back key never quits; in flight it opens the menu (user decision).
- Rental return: the core rule (an active rental can only be returned where an own ship is docked to switch into) is unchanged, but the reason is now an offer note shown under the disabled button. Renting charges fee + deposit + first day at once (`Hangar.RentalUpfront`, `RentPaidDays` = 1); taxi and rent offers show the whole amount with a breakdown (displayed price = deduction, tests).
- `SpaceSim.Core/Ui/MessageRules`: important (about you or your fight, large toast/alert), info (`Hud.Info`: small, dimmed, never replaces an important toast — foreign events in your sector), off (elsewhere). `EnemyExit` gives a message for every way an enemy leaves your fight.
- Hamburger menu: music/effects keep it open; a new UI factor reaches the menu layer only after the menu closes, so nothing moves under the finger. Selftest stage 114 (`SelfTest.Round38a.cs`), tests `Runde38aTests`.
